});\n332\t});\n333\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3700803d-47a8-4d4a-84c0-cfce77f561c0","uuid":"c3846faf-99d0-492d-a2f4-477340bf823c","timestamp":"2026-10-09T09:36:42.175Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/scheduling/__tests__/WeeklySchedulePage.spec.tsx","content":"/**\n * WeeklySchedulePage — the roster reads and writes the scheduling API.\n *\n * HR-HIGH-010: \"Kaydet\" wrote the roster to localStorage and nothing reached\n * the API. These tests pin the replacement: the cells come from the team\n * weekly overview (one query per visible week), a choice in a cell calls the\n * assignment mutation with the employee's plan — or none, so the API creates\n * one — published weeks and leave days take no assignment, publishing goes\n * through the confirm gate, and browser storage is never written.\n */\nimport { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\nimport type { Shift } from '../../../types/attendance.types';\nimport type {\n  DayEntry,\n  EmployeeWeekSummary,\n  TeamWeeklyOverview,\n  WeekDay,\n} from '../../../types/scheduling.types';\nimport { WeeklySchedulePage } from '../WeeklySchedulePage';\n\nconst WEEKDAYS: readonly WeekDay[] = [\n  'monday',\n  'tuesday',\n  'wednesday',\n  'thursday',\n  'friday',\n  'saturday',\n  'sunday',\n];\n\ntype DayPattern = 'S' | 'A' | 'off' | 'leave';\n\n/** A day of the week of 2026-09-14, as the overview query returns it. */\nfunction entryOn(weekday: WeekDay, index: number, pattern: DayPattern): DayEntry {\n  const date = `2026-09-${String(14 + index).padStart(2, '0')}`;\n  if (pattern === 'off' || pattern === 'leave') {\n    return { dayOfWeek: weekday, date, entryType: pattern, plannedMinutes: 0 };\n  }\n  return {\n    dayOfWeek: weekday,\n    date,\n    entryType: 'work',\n    shiftCode: pattern,\n    shiftName: pattern === 'S' ? 'Sabah' : 'Akşam',\n    startTime: pattern === 'S' ? '08:00:00' : '16:00:00',\n    endTime: pattern === 'S' ? '16:00:00' : '00:00:00',\n    plannedMinutes: 480,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction week(pattern: readonly DayPattern[]): DayEntry[] {\n  return WEEKDAYS.map((weekday, index) => entryOn(weekday, index, pattern[index] ?? 'off'));\n}\n\nfunction overviewFor(weekStartDate: string): TeamWeeklyOverview {\n  const employeePlans: EmployeeWeekSummary[] = [\n    {\n      employeeId: 'e-ayse',\n      employeeName: 'Ayşe Yılmaz',\n      position: 'Teknisyen',\n      weeklyPlanId: 'plan-ayse',\n      planStatus: 'draft',\n      days: week(['S', 'S', 'S', 'S', 'S', 'off', 'off']),\n      totalWorkDays: 5,\n      totalMinutes: 2400,\n      overtimeMinutes: 0,\n    },\n    {\n      employeeId: 'e-mehmet',\n      employeeName: 'Mehmet Kaya',\n      position: 'Vardiya Amiri',\n      weeklyPlanId: 'plan-mehmet',\n      planStatus: 'published',\n      days: week(['A', 'A', 'A', 'A', 'A', 'off', 'off']),\n      totalWorkDays: 5,\n      totalMinutes: 2400,\n      overtimeMinutes: 0,\n    },\n    {\n      // No plan for the week: the API reports seven off days and no plan id.\n      employeeId: 'e-fatma',\n      employeeName: 'Fatma Demir',\n      days: week(['off', 'off', 'off', 'off', 'off', 'off', 'off']),\n      totalWorkDays: 0,\n      totalMinutes: 0,\n      overtimeMinutes: 0,\n    },\n    {\n      employeeId: 'e-ali',\n      employeeName: 'Ali Çelik',\n      position: 'Bakımcı',\n      weeklyPlanId: 'plan-ali',\n      planStatus: 'draft',\n      days: week(['S', 'S', 'leave', 'S', 'S', 'off', 'off']),\n      totalWorkDays: 4,\n      totalMinutes: 1920,\n      overtimeMinutes: 0,\n    },\n  ];\n  return {\n    weekStartDate,\n    weekEndDate: '2026-09-20',\n    totalEmployees: employeePlans.length,\n    employeePlans,\n    daysSummary: [],\n  };\n}\n\nconst mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({\n  overviews: vi.fn(),\n  assignMutate: vi.fn(),\n  publishMutate: vi.fn(),\n  confirm: vi.fn<(options: unknown) => Promise<boolean>>(),\n}));\n\nconst SHIFTS: Shift[] = [\n  {\n    id: 'shift-s',\n    tenantId: 't-1',\n    code: 'S',\n    name: 'Sabah',\n    startTime: '08:00:00',\n    endTime: '16:00:00',\n    graceMinutes: 15,\n    breakPeriods: [],\n    workDays: [],\n    isNightShift: false,\n    isOffshoreShift: false,\n    colorCode: '#0ea5e9',\n    isActive: true,\n    isDeleted: false,\n    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n  },\n  {\n    id: 'shift-a',\n    tenantId: 't-1',\n    code: 'A',\n    name: 'Akşam',\n    startTime: '16:00:00',\n    endTime: '00:00:00',\n    graceMinutes: 15,\n    breakPeriods: [],\n    workDays: [],\n    isNightShift: true,\n    isOffshoreShift: false,\n    colorCode: '#6366f1',\n    isActive: true,\n    isDeleted: false,\n    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n  },\n];\n\nvi.mock('../../../hooks/useScheduling', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useScheduling')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    useTeamWeeklyOverviews: mocks.overviews,\n    useAssignScheduleCell: () => ({\n      mutate: mocks.assignMutate,\n      isPending: false,\n      variables: undefined,\n    }),\n    usePublishWeekPlans: () => ({ mutate: mocks.publishMutate, isPending: false }),\n  };\n});\n\nvi.mock('../../../hooks/useAttendance', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useAttendance')>();\n  return { ...actual, useShifts: () => ({ data: SHIFTS, isPending: false }) };\n});\n\nvi.mock('@aquaculture/shared-ui', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('@aquaculture/shared-ui')>();\n  return { ...actual, useConfirm: () => mocks.confirm };\n});\n\nfunction renderPage(): void {\n  render(\n    <MemoryRouter>\n      <WeeklySchedulePage />\n    </MemoryRouter>,\n  );\n}\n\nfunction rowOf(employeeName: string): HTMLElement {\n  const row = screen.getByText(employeeName).closest('tr');\n  if (row === null) throw new Error(`No roster row for ${employeeName}`);\n  return row;\n}\n\nfunction nth<T>(list: readonly T[], index: number): T {\n  const item = list[index];\n  if (item === undefined) throw new Error(`No item at ${index}`);\n  return item;\n}\n\ndescribe('WeeklySchedulePage', () => {\n  let setItem: ReturnType<typeof vi.spyOn>;\n\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] });\n    vi.setSystemTime(new Date(2026, 8, 16, 10, 0, 0)); // Wednesday → the week of Monday 2026-09-14\n    mocks.overviews.mockImplementation((weekStarts: readonly string[]) =>\n      weekStarts.map((weekStartDate) => ({\n        data: overviewFor(weekStartDate),\n        isError: false,\n        error: null,\n        refetch: vi.fn(),\n      })),\n    );\n    mocks.assignMutate.mockReset();\n    mocks.publishMutate.mockReset();\n    mocks.confirm.mockReset();\n    mocks.confirm.mockResolvedValue(true);\n    setItem = vi.spyOn(Storage.prototype, 'setItem');\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => {\n    vi.useRealTimers();\n    vi.restoreAllMocks();\n  });\n\n  it('renders the week from the team overview: shift codes, off days, leave and unplanned cells', () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    expect(mocks.overviews).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(['2026-09-14']);\n    const ayse = rowOf('Ayşe Yılmaz');\n    expect(within(ayse).getAllByText('S')).toHaveLength(5);\n    expect(within(ayse).getAllByText('Tatil')).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(within(rowOf('Ali Çelik')).getByText('İzin')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(within(rowOf('Fatma Demir')).queryByText('Tatil')).toBeNull();\n    expect(within(rowOf('Mehmet Kaya')).getByText('Yayınlandı')).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it('assigns a shift to a cell through the API with the employee’s plan', () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    fireEvent.click(nth(within(rowOf('Ayşe Yılmaz')).getAllByRole('button'), 2));\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: /Akşam/ }));\n\n    expect(mocks.assignMutate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n      employeeId: 'e-ayse',\n      weekStartDate: '2026-09-14',\n      weeklyPlanId: 'plan-ayse',\n      date: '2026-09-16',\n      shiftId: 'shift-a',\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('asks the API to create the plan first for an employee without one', () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    fireEvent.click(nth(within(rowOf('Fatma Demir')).getAllByRole('button'), 0));\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: /Sabah/ }));\n\n    expect(mocks.assignMutate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n      employeeId: 'e-fatma',\n      weekStartDate: '2026-09-14',\n      weeklyPlanId: undefined,\n      date: '2026-09-14',\n      shiftId: 'shift-s',\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('marks an off day with a null shift', () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    fireEvent.click(nth(within(rowOf('Ayşe Yılmaz')).getAllByRole('button'), 0));\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Tatil' }));\n\n    expect(mocks.assignMutate).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n      expect.objectContaining({ employeeId: 'e-ayse', date: '2026-09-14', shiftId: null }),\n    );\n  });\n\n  it('takes no assignment on a published week or a leave day', () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    expect(within(rowOf('Mehmet Kaya')).queryAllByRole('button')).toHaveLength(0);\n    expect(within(rowOf('Ali Çelik')).getAllByRole('button')).toHaveLength(6);\n  });\n\n  it('publishes the week’s draft plans behind the confirm gate', async () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Haftayı Yayınla \\(2\\)/ }));\n\n    await vi.waitFor(() =>\n      expect(mocks.publishMutate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n        weekStartDate: '2026-09-14',\n        planIds: ['plan-ayse', 'plan-ali'],\n      }),\n    );\n    expect(mocks.confirm).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n  });\n\n  it('publishes nothing when the confirm gate is refused', async () => {\n    mocks.confirm.mockResolvedValue(false);\n    renderPage();\n\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Haftayı Yayınla \\(2\\)/ }));\n\n    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(mocks.confirm).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));\n    expect(mocks.publishMutate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it('reads the month as one overview per week', () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aylık' }));\n\n    expect(mocks.overviews).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith([\n      '2026-08-31',\n      '2026-09-07',\n      '2026-09-14',\n      '2026-09-21',\n      '2026-09-28',\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it('writes nothing to browser storage and offers no local save', () => {\n    renderPage();\n\n    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Kaydet' })).toBeNull();\n    expect(setItem).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n});\n","numLines":333,"startLine":1,"totalLines":333}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NA4cWdvVnvUJX9LTGNt2VK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Leave Management Hooks\n3\t * TanStack Query hooks for leave operations\n4\t */\n5\t\n6\timport { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\n7\timport { useAuth, createTenantQueryKey, createTenantInvalidationKey, useFeedbackMutation } from '@aquaculture/shared-ui';\n8\timport { useGraphQLClient, graphqlRequest } from './useGraphQL';\n9\timport {\n10\t  GET_LEAVE_TYPES,\n11\t  GET_LEAVE_BALANCES,\n12\t  GET_LEAVE_BALANCE_SUMMARY,\n13\t  GET_LEAVE_REQUESTS,\n14\t  GET_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n15\t  GET_MY_LEAVE_REQUESTS,\n16\t  GET_PENDING_LEAVE_APPROVALS,\n17\t  GET_TEAM_LEAVE_CALENDAR,\n18\t  CHECK_LEAVE_OVERLAP,\n19\t  CALCULATE_LEAVE_DAYS,\n20\t  CREATE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n21\t  UPDATE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n22\t  SUBMIT_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n23\t  APPROVE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n24\t  REJECT_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n25\t  CANCEL_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n26\t  WITHDRAW_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n27\t  ADJUST_LEAVE_BALANCE,\n28\t} from '../graphql';\n29\timport type {\n30\t  LeaveType,\n31\t  LeaveBalance,\n32\t  LeaveRequest,\n33\t  LeaveCalendarEntry,\n34\t  LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n35\t  CreateLeaveRequestInput,\n36\t  UpdateLeaveRequestInput,\n37\t  PaginationInput,\n38\t  PaginatedResponse,\n39\t} from '../types';\n40\t\n41\t// Query Keys\n42\texport const leaveKeys = {\n43\t  all: ['leaves'] as const,\n44\t  types: () => [...leaveKeys.all, 'types'] as const,\n45\t  balances: (employeeId: string, year: number) =>\n46\t    [...leaveKeys.all, 'balances', employeeId, year] as const,\n47\t  balanceSummary: (employeeId: string, year: number) =>\n48\t    [...leaveKeys.all, 'balanceSummary', employeeId, year] as const,\n49\t  requests: () => [...leaveKeys.all, 'requests'] as const,\n50\t  requestList: (filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput, pagination?: PaginationInput) =>\n51\t    [...leaveKeys.requests(), { filter, pagination }] as const,\n52\t  requestDetail: (id: string) => [...leaveKeys.requests(), id] as const,\n53\t  myRequests: (filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput, pagination?: PaginationInput) =>\n54\t    [...leaveKeys.all, 'myRequests', { filter, pagination }] as const,\n55\t  pendingApprovals: (approverId: string) =>\n56\t    [...leaveKeys.all, 'pendingApprovals', approverId] as const,\n57\t  calendar: (departmentId: string | undefined, startDate: string, endDate: string) =>\n58\t    [...leaveKeys.all, 'calendar', departmentId, startDate, endDate] as const,\n59\t};\n60\t\n61\t// =====================\n62\t// Leave Type Queries\n63\t// =====================\n64\t\n65\texport function useLeaveTypes(filter?: { category?: string; isActive?: boolean }) {\n66\t  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n67\t  return useQuery({\n68\t    queryKey: leaveKeys.types(),\n69\t    queryFn: () =>\n70\t      graphqlRequest<{ leaveTypes: LeaveType[] }, unknown>(\n71\t        client,\n72\t        GET_LEAVE_TYPES,\n73\t        { filter }\n74\t      ),\n75\t    select: (data) => data.leaveTypes,\n76\t  });\n77\t}\n78\t\n79\t// =====================\n80\t// Leave Balance Queries\n81\t// =====================\n82\t\n83\texport function useLeaveBalances(employeeId: string, year: number) {\n84\t  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n85\t\n86\t  return useQuery({\n87\t    queryKey: leaveKeys.balances(employeeId, year),\n88\t    queryFn: () =>\n89\t      graphqlRequest<{ leaveBalances: LeaveBalance[] }, unknown>(\n90\t        client,\n91\t        GET_LEAVE_BALANCES,\n92\t        { employeeId, year }\n93\t      ),\n94\t    select: (data) => data.leaveBalances,\n95\t    // BUG-005: year=0 is falsy but a valid-looking bad value; guard with range check\n96\t    enabled: !!employeeId && year > 2000 && year < 2100,\n97\t  });\n98\t}\n99\t\n100\texport function useLeaveBalanceSummary(employeeId: string, year: number) {\n101\t  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n102\t  return useQuery({\n103\t    queryKey: leaveKeys.balanceSummary(employeeId, year),\n104\t    queryFn: () =>\n105\t      graphqlRequest<{\n106\t        leaveBalanceSummary: {\n107\t          totalEntitled: number;\n108\t          totalUsed: number;\n109\t          totalPending: number;\n110\t          totalAvailable: number;\n111\t          balances: {\n112\t            leaveTypeId: string;\n113\t            leaveTypeName: string;\n114\t            leaveTypeColor: string;\n115\t            entitled: number;\n116\t            used: number;\n117\t            pending: number;\n118\t            available: number;\n119\t          }[];\n120\t        };\n121\t      }, unknown>(client, GET_LEAVE_BALANCE_SUMMARY, {\n122\t        employeeId,\n123\t        year,\n124\t      }),\n125\t    select: (data) => data.leaveBalanceSummary,\n126\t    // BUG-005: match the same range guard used by useLeaveBalances so year=0 or\n127\t    // NaN values cannot trigger an erroneous fetch.\n128\t    enabled: !!employeeId && year > 2000 && year < 2100,\n129\t  });\n130\t}\n131\t\n132\t// =====================\n133\t// Leave Request Queries\n134\t// =====================\n135\t\n136\texport function useLeaveRequests(\n137\t  filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n138\t  pagination?: PaginationInput\n139\t) {\n140\t  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n141\t\n142\t  return useQuery({\n143\t    queryKey: leaveKeys.requestList(filter, pagination),\n144\t    queryFn: () =>\n145\t      graphqlRequest<{ leaveRequests: PaginatedResponse<LeaveRequest> }, unknown>(\n146\t        client,\n147\t        GET_LEAVE_REQUESTS,\n148\t        { filter, pagination }\n149\t      ),\n150\t    select: (data) => data.leaveRequests,\n151\t  });\n152\t}\n153\t\n154\texport function useLeaveRequest(id: string) {\n155\t  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n156\t\n157\t  const { tenantId } = useAuth();\n158\t  return useQuery({\n159\t    queryKey: leaveKeys.requestDetail(id),\n160\t    queryFn: () =>\n161\t      graphqlRequest<{ leaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n162\t        client,\n163\t        GET_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n164\t        { id }\n165\t      ),\n166\t    select: (data) => data.leaveRequest,\n167\t    enabled: !!id && !!tenantId,\n168\t  });\n169\t}\n170\t\n171\texport function useMyLeaveRequests(\n172\t  filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n173\t  pagination?: PaginationInput\n174\t) {\n175\t  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n176\t\n177\t  return useQuery({\n178\t    queryKey: leaveKeys.myRequests(filter, pagination),\n179\t    queryFn: () =>\n180\t      graphqlRequest<{ myLeaveRequests: PaginatedResponse<LeaveRequest> }, unknown>(\n181\t        client,\n182\t        GET_MY_LEAVE_REQUESTS,\n183\t        { filter, pagination }\n184\t      ),\n185\t    select: (data) => data.myLeaveRequests,\n186\t  });\n187\t}\n188\t\n189\texport function usePendingLeaveApprovals(approverId: string) {\n190\t  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n191\t  return useQuery({\n192\t    queryKey: leaveKeys.pendingApprovals(approverId),\n193\t    queryFn: () =>\n194\t      graphqlRequest<{ pendingLeaveApprovals: LeaveRequest[] }, unknown>(\n195\t        client,\n196\t        GET_PENDING_LEAVE_APPROVALS,\n197\t        { approverId }\n198\t      ),\n199\t    select: (data) => data.pendingLeaveApprovals,\n200\t    enabled: !!approverId,\n201\t  });\n202\t}\n203\t\n204\texport function useTeamLeaveCalendar(\n205\t  departmentId: string | undefined,\n206\t  startDate: string,\n207\t  endDate: string\n208\t) {\n209\t  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n210\t\n211\t  return useQuery({\n212\t    queryKey: leaveKeys.calendar(departmentId, startDate, * Leave Management Hooks\n * TanStack Query hooks for leave operations\n */\n\nimport { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\nimport { useAuth, createTenantQueryKey, createTenantInvalidationKey, useFeedbackMutation } from '@aquaculture/shared-ui';\nimport { useGraphQLClient, graphqlRequest } from './useGraphQL';\nimport {\n  GET_LEAVE_TYPES,\n  GET_LEAVE_BALANCES,\n  GET_LEAVE_BALANCE_SUMMARY,\n  GET_LEAVE_REQUESTS,\n  GET_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n  GET_MY_LEAVE_REQUESTS,\n  GET_PENDING_LEAVE_APPROVALS,\n  GET_TEAM_LEAVE_CALENDAR,\n  CHECK_LEAVE_OVERLAP,\n  CALCULATE_LEAVE_DAYS,\n  CREATE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n  UPDATE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n  SUBMIT_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n  APPROVE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n  REJECT_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n  CANCEL_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n  WITHDRAW_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n  ADJUST_LEAVE_BALANCE,\n} from '../graphql';\nimport type {\n  LeaveType,\n  LeaveBalance,\n  LeaveRequest,\n  LeaveCalendarEntry,\n  LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n  CreateLeaveRequestInput,\n  UpdateLeaveRequestInput,\n  PaginationInput,\n  PaginatedResponse,\n} from '../types';\n\n// Query Keys\nexport const leaveKeys = {\n  all: ['leaves'] as const,\n  types: () => [...leaveKeys.all, 'types'] as const,\n  balances: (employeeId: string, year: number) =>\n    [...leaveKeys.all, 'balances', employeeId, year] as const,\n  balanceSummary: (employeeId: string, year: number) =>\n    [...leaveKeys.all, 'balanceSummary', employeeId, year] as const,\n  requests: () => [...leaveKeys.all, 'requests'] as const,\n  requestList: (filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput, pagination?: PaginationInput) =>\n    [...leaveKeys.requests(), { filter, pagination }] as const,\n  requestDetail: (id: string) => [...leaveKeys.requests(), id] as const,\n  myRequests: (filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput, pagination?: PaginationInput) =>\n    [...leaveKeys.all, 'myRequests', { filter, pagination }] as const,\n  pendingApprovals: (approverId: string) =>\n    [...leaveKeys.all, 'pendingApprovals', approverId] as const,\n  calendar: (departmentId: string | undefined, startDate: string, endDate: string) =>\n    [...leaveKeys.all, 'calendar', departmentId, startDate, endDate] as const,\n};\n\n// =====================\n// Leave Type Queries\n// =====================\n\nexport function useLeaveTypes(filter?: { category?: string; isActive?: boolean }) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: leaveKeys.types(),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ leaveTypes: LeaveType[] }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_LEAVE_TYPES,\n        { filter }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.leaveTypes,\n  });\n}\n\n// =====================\n// Leave Balance Queries\n// =====================\n\nexport function useLeaveBalances(employeeId: string, year: number) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: leaveKeys.balances(employeeId, year),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ leaveBalances: LeaveBalance[] }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_LEAVE_BALANCES,\n        { employeeId, year }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.leaveBalances,\n    // BUG-005: year=0 is falsy but a valid-looking bad value; guard with range check\n    enabled: !!employeeId && year > 2000 && year < 2100,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useLeaveBalanceSummary(employeeId: string, year: number) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: leaveKeys.balanceSummary(employeeId, year),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{\n        leaveBalanceSummary: {\n          totalEntitled: number;\n          totalUsed: number;\n          totalPending: number;\n          totalAvailable: number;\n          balances: {\n            leaveTypeId: string;\n            leaveTypeName: string;\n            leaveTypeColor: string;\n            entitled: number;\n            used: number;\n            pending: number;\n            available: number;\n          }[];\n        };\n      }, unknown>(client, GET_LEAVE_BALANCE_SUMMARY, {\n        employeeId,\n        year,\n      }),\n    select: (data) => data.leaveBalanceSummary,\n    // BUG-005: match the same range guard used by useLeaveBalances so year=0 or\n    // NaN values cannot trigger an erroneous fetch.\n    enabled: !!employeeId && year > 2000 && year < 2100,\n  });\n}\n\n// =====================\n// Leave Request Queries\n// =====================\n\nexport function useLeaveRequests(\n  filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n  pagination?: PaginationInput\n) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: leaveKeys.requestList(filter, pagination),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ leaveRequests: PaginatedResponse<LeaveRequest> }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_LEAVE_REQUESTS,\n        { filter, pagination }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.leaveRequests,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useLeaveRequest(id: string) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  const { tenantId } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: leaveKeys.requestDetail(id),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ leaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n        { id }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.leaveRequest,\n    enabled: !!id && !!tenantId,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useMyLeaveRequests(\n  filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n  pagination?: PaginationInput\n) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: leaveKeys.myRequests(filter, pagination),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ myLeaveRequests: PaginatedResponse<LeaveRequest> }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_MY_LEAVE_REQUESTS,\n        { filter, pagination }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.myLeaveRequests,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function usePendingLeaveApprovals(approverId: string) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: leaveKeys.pendingApprovals(approverId),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ pendingLeaveApprovals: LeaveRequest[] }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_PENDING_LEAVE_APPROVALS,\n        { approverId }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.pendingLeaveApprovals,\n    enabled: !!approverId,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useTeamLeaveCalendar(\n  departmentId: string | undefined,\n  startDate: string,\n  endDate: string\n) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: leaveKeys.calendar(departmentId, startDate, endDate),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ teamLeaveCalendar: LeaveCalendarEntry[] }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_TEAM_LEAVE_CALENDAR,\n        { departmentId, startDate, endDate }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.teamLeaveCalendar,\n    enabled: !!startDate && !!endDate,\n  });\n}\n\n// =====================\n// Leave Validation Queries\n// =====================\n\nexport function useCheckLeaveOverlap(\n  employeeId: string,\n  startDate: string,\n  endDate: string,\n  excludeRequestId?: string\n) {\n  const { tenantId } = useAuth();\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: createTenantQueryKey(tenantId, 'leaveOverlap', employeeId, startDate, endDate, excludeRequestId),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{\n        checkLeaveOverlap: {\n          hasOverlap: boolean;\n          overlappingRequests: { id: string; requestNumber: string; startDate: string; endDate: string; status: string }[];\n        };\n      }, unknown>(client, CHECK_LEAVE_OVERLAP, {\n        employeeId,\n        startDate,\n        endDate,\n        excludeRequestId,\n      }),\n    select: (data) => data.checkLeaveOverlap,\n    enabled: !!employeeId && !!startDate && !!endDate,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useCalculateLeaveDays(\n  leaveTypeId: string,\n  startDate: string,\n  endDate: string,\n  options?: { isHalfDayStart?: boolean; isHalfDayEnd?: boolean }\n) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const { tenantId } = useAuth();\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: createTenantQueryKey(tenantId, 'leaveDays', leaveTypeId, startDate, endDate, options),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{\n        calculateLeaveDays: {\n          totalDays: number;\n          workingDays: number;\n          weekends: number;\n          holidays: number;\n        };\n      }, unknown>(client, CALCULATE_LEAVE_DAYS, {\n        leaveTypeId,\n        startDate,\n        endDate,\n        ...options,\n      }),\n    select: (data) => data.calculateLeaveDays,\n    enabled: !!leaveTypeId && !!startDate && !!endDate,\n  });\n}\n\n// =====================\n// Leave Request Mutations\n// =====================\n\nexport function useCreateLeaveRequest() {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useFeedbackMutation({\n    feedback: { success: 'Leave request created' },\n    mutationFn: (input: CreateLeaveRequestInput) =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ createLeaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        CREATE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n        { input }\n      ),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.requests() });\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.myRequests() });\n      // Update balances if pending was increased\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({\n        queryKey: leaveKeys.balances(data.createLeaveRequest.employeeId, new Date().getFullYear()),\n      });\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useUpdateLeaveRequest() {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n\n  return useFeedbackMutation({\n    feedback: { success: 'Leave request updated' },\n    mutationFn: (input: UpdateLeaveRequestInput) =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ updateLeaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        UPDATE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n        { input }\n      ),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.requests() });\n      queryClient.setQueryData(\n        leaveKeys.requestDetail(data.updateLeaveRequest.id),\n        data.updateLeaveRequest\n      );\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useSubmitLeaveRequest() {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useFeedbackMutation({\n    feedback: { success: 'Leave request submitted' },\n    mutationFn: (id: string) =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ submitLeaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        SUBMIT_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n        { id }\n      ),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.requests() });\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.myRequests() });\n      queryClient.setQueryData(\n        leaveKeys.requestDetail(data.submitLeaveRequest.id),\n        data.submitLeaveRequest\n      );\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useApproveLeaveRequest() {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  const { tenantId } = useAuth();\n\n  return useFeedbackMutation({\n    feedback: { success: 'Leave request approved' },\n    mutationFn: ({ id, notes }: { id: string; notes?: string }) =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ approveLeaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        APPROVE_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n        { id, notes }\n      ),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.requests() });\n      // BUG-014: use prefix invalidation so all pendingApprovals(approverId) keys are invalidated\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({\n        queryKey: createTenantInvalidationKey(tenantId, ...leaveKeys.all, 'pendingApprovals'),\n        exact: false,\n      });\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({\n        queryKey: leaveKeys.balances(data.approveLeaveRequest.employeeId, new Date().getFullYear()),\n      });\n      queryClient.setQueryData(\n        leaveKeys.requestDetail(data.approveLeaveRequest.id),\n        data.approveLeaveRequest\n      );\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useRejectLeaveRequest() {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n\n  const { tenantId } = useAuth();\n  return useFeedbackMutation({\n    feedback: { success: 'Leave request rejected' },\n    mutationFn: ({ id, reason }: { id: string; reason: string }) =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ rejectLeaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        REJECT_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n        { id, reason }\n      ),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.requests() });\n      // BUG-014: use prefix invalidation so all pendingApprovals(approverId) keys are invalidated\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({\n        queryKey: createTenantInvalidationKey(tenantId, ...leaveKeys.all, 'pendingApprovals'),\n        exact: false,\n      });\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({\n        queryKey: leaveKeys.balances(data.rejectLeaveRequest.employeeId, new Date().getFullYear()),\n      });\n      queryClient.setQueryData(\n        leaveKeys.requestDetail(data.rejectLeaveRequest.id),\n        data.rejectLeaveRequest\n      );\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useCancelLeaveRequest() {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useFeedbackMutation({\n    feedback: { success: 'Leave request cancelled' },\n    mutationFn: ({ id, reason }: { id: string; reason?: string }) =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ cancelLeaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        CANCEL_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n        { id, reason }\n      ),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.requests() });\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.myRequests() });\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({\n        queryKey: leaveKeys.balances(data.cancelLeaveRequest.employeeId, new Date().getFullYear()),\n      });\n      queryClient.setQueryData(\n        leaveKeys.requestDetail(data.cancelLeaveRequest.id),\n        data.cancelLeaveRequest\n      );\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useWithdrawLeaveRequest() {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useFeedbackMutation({\n    feedback: { success: 'Leave request withdrawn' },\n    mutationFn: (id: string) =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ withdrawLeaveRequest: LeaveRequest }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        WITHDRAW_LEAVE_REQUEST,\n        { id }\n      ),\n    onSuccess: (data) => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.requests() });\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: leaveKeys.myRequests() });\n      queryClient.setQueryData(\n        leaveKeys.requestDetail(data.withdrawLeaveRequest.id),\n        data.withdrawLeaveRequest\n      );\n    },\n  });\n}\n\n// =====================\n// Leave Balance Mutations\n// =====================\n\nexport function useAdjustLeaveBalance() {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  return useFeedbackMutation({\n    feedback: { success: 'Leave balance adjusted' },\n    mutationFn: ({\n      employeeId,\n      leaveTypeId,\n      year,\n      adjustment,\n      reason,\n    }: {\n      employeeId: string;\n      leaveTypeId: string;\n      year: number;\n      adjustment: number;\n      reason: string;\n    }) =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ adjustLeaveBalance: LeaveBalance }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        ADJUST_LEAVE_BALANCE,\n        { employeeId, leaveTypeId, year, adjustment, reason }\n      ),\n    onSuccess: (_, variables) => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({\n        queryKey: leaveKeys.balances(variables.employeeId, variables.year),\n      });\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({\n        queryKey: leaveKeys.balanceSummary(variables.employeeId, variables.year),\n      });\n    },\n  });\n}\n","numLines":502,"startLine":1,"totalLines":502}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WAgBYCH3FiD5j75tGzesiS","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * New leave request (FE-MEDIUM-092) — the action behind the leave page's\n3\t * \"New Request\" control, which rendered with no handler. Employee, leave type\n4\t * and dates are chosen here; the day count comes from the same server\n5\t * calculation the balances use, and the request is created through the\n6\t * existing mutation hook, whose toast reports the outcome.\n7\t */\n8\t\n9\timport React, { useState } from 'react';\n10\timport { Button, FormField, Input, Modal, Select, Textarea } from '@aquaculture/shared-ui';\n11\t\n12\timport {\n13\t  useCalculateLeaveDays,\n14\t  useCreateLeaveRequest,\n15\t  useEmployees,\n16\t  useLeaveTypes,\n17\t} from '../../hooks';\n18\timport type { Employee } from '../../types';\n19\t\n20\tconst employeeLabel = (employee: Employee): string =>\n21\t  `${employee.firstName} ${employee.lastName} (${employee.employeeNumber})`;\n22\t\n23\tconst EMPTY = { employeeId: '', leaveTypeId: '', startDate: '', endDate: '', reason: '' };\n24\t\n25\texport interface NewLeaveRequestModalProps {\n26\t  open: boolean;\n27\t  onClose: () => void;\n28\t}\n29\t\n30\texport const NewLeaveRequestModal: React.FC<NewLeaveRequestModalProps> = ({ open, onClose }) => {\n31\t  const { data: employees } = useEmployees({}, { limit: 1000 });\n32\t  const { data: leaveTypes } = useLeaveTypes({ isActive: true });\n33\t  const create = useCreateLeaveRequest();\n34\t  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);\n35\t  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});\n36\t  const datesValid = Boolean(form.startDate && form.endDate && form.endDate >= form.startDate);\n37\t  const days = useCalculateLeaveDays(\n38\t    form.leaveTypeId,\n39\t    datesValid ? form.startDate : '',\n40\t    datesValid ? form.endDate : '',\n41\t  );\n42\t\n43\t  const close = (): void => {\n44\t    setForm(EMPTY);\n45\t    setErrors({});\n46\t    onClose();\n47\t  };\n48\t\n49\t  const handleSubmit = (event: React.FormEvent): void => {\n50\t    event.preventDefault();\n51\t    const next: Record<string, string> = {};\n52\t    if (!form.employeeId) next.employeeId = 'Choose an employee';\n53\t    if (!form.leaveTypeId) next.leaveTypeId = 'Choose a leave type';\n54\t    if (!form.startDate) next.startDate = 'Enter the first day';\n55\t    if (!form.endDate) next.endDate = 'Enter the last day';\n56\t    if (form.startDate && form.endDate && form.endDate < form.startDate)\n57\t      next.endDate = 'The last day is before the first day';\n58\t    setErrors(next);\n59\t    if (Object.keys(next).length > 0 || !days.data) return;\n60\t    create.mutate(\n61\t      {\n62\t        employeeId: form.employeeId,\n63\t        leaveTypeId: form.leaveTypeId,\n64\t        startDate: form.startDate,\n65\t        endDate: form.endDate,\n66\t        totalDays: days.data.totalDays,\n67\t        reason: form.reason.trim() || undefined,\n68\t      },\n69\t      { onSuccess: close },\n70\t    );"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3700803d-47a8-4d4a-84c0-cfce77f561c0","uuid":"87ddf6d6-a0a4-49d2-921f-f7b0a88eafc1","timestamp":"2026-10-09T09:37:47.640Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678/web/modules/hr-module/src/components/leave/NewLeaveRequestModal.tsx","content":"/**\n * New leave request (FE-MEDIUM-092) — the action behind the leave page's\n * \"New Request\" control, which rendered with no handler. Employee, leave type\n * and dates are chosen here; the day count comes from the same server\n * calculation the balances use, and the request is created through the\n * existing mutation hook, whose toast reports the outcome.\n */\n\nimport React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport { Button, FormField, Input, Modal, Select, Textarea } from '@aquaculture/shared-ui';\n\nimport {\n  useCalculateLeaveDays,\n  useCreateLeaveRequest,\n  useEmployees,\n  useLeaveTypes,\n} from '../../hooks';\nimport type { Employee } from '../../types';\n\nconst employeeLabel = (employee: Employee): string =>\n  `${employee.firstName} ${employee.lastName} (${employee.employeeNumber})`;\n\nconst EMPTY = { employeeId: '', leaveTypeId: '', startDate: '', endDate: '', reason: '' };\n\nexport interface NewLeaveRequestModalProps {\n  open: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n}\n\nexport const NewLeaveRequestModal: React.FC<NewLeaveRequestModalProps> = ({ open, onClose }) => {\n  const { data: employees } = useEmployees({}, { limit: 1000 });\n  const { data: leaveTypes } = useLeaveTypes({ isActive: true });\n  const create = useCreateLeaveRequest();\n  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);\n  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});\n  const datesValid = Boolean(form.startDate && form.endDate && form.endDate >= form.startDate);\n  const days = useCalculateLeaveDays(\n    form.leaveTypeId,\n    datesValid ? form.startDate : '',\n    datesValid ? form.endDate : '',\n  );\n\n  const close = (): void => {\n    setForm(EMPTY);\n    setErrors({});\n    onClose();\n  };\n\n  const handleSubmit = (event: React.FormEvent): void => {\n    event.preventDefault();\n    const next: Record<string, string> = {};\n    if (!form.employeeId) next.employeeId = 'Choose an employee';\n    if (!form.leaveTypeId) next.leaveTypeId = 'Choose a leave type';\n    if (!form.startDate) next.startDate = 'Enter the first day';\n    if (!form.endDate) next.endDate = 'Enter the last day';\n    if (form.startDate && form.endDate && form.endDate < form.startDate)\n      next.endDate = 'The last day is before the first day';\n    setErrors(next);\n    if (Object.keys(next).length > 0 || !days.data) return;\n    create.mutate(\n      {\n        employeeId: form.employeeId,\n        leaveTypeId: form.leaveTypeId,\n        startDate: form.startDate,\n        endDate: form.endDate,\n        totalDays: days.data.totalDays,\n        reason: form.reason.trim() || undefined,\n      },\n      { onSuccess: close },\n    );","numLines":70,"startLine":1,"totalLines":187}}}

page?: number }\n) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: employeeKeys.list({ ...filter, ...pagination }),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ employees: PaginatedResponse<Employee> }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_EMPLOYEES,\n        {\n          filter: filter || undefined,\n          pagination: pagination || undefined,\n        }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.employees,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useEmployee(id: string) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  const { tenantId } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: employeeKeys.detail(id),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ employee: Employee }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_EMPLOYEE,\n        { id }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.employee,\n    enabled: !!id && !!tenantId,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useEmployeeByNumber(employeeNumber: string) {\n  const client = useGraphQLClient();\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: employeeKeys.byNumber(employeeNumber),\n    queryFn: () =>\n      graphqlRequest<{ employees: PaginatedResponse<Employee> }, unknown>(\n        client,\n        GET_EMPLOYEE_BY_NUMBER,\n        { filter: { employeeNumber } }\n      ),\n    select: (data) => data.employees?.items?.[0],\n    enabled: !!employeeNumber,\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * PERF-006: callers should debounce the `search` argument before passing it in\n * (e.g. with useDeferredValue or a 300ms setTimeout) so each keystroke does not\n * fire a new network request. Module scope\n// keeps the array identity stable across renders.\nconst LEAVE_STATUS_FILTER_OPTIONS = [\n  { value: '', label: 'All Statuses' },\n  ...Object.entries(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG).map(([status, config]) => ({\n    value: status,\n    label: config.label,\n  })),\n];\n\n// F-015: a checked narrowing grounded in the same record, so it cannot drift from\n// the option list above.\nconst isLeaveRequestStatus = (value: string): value is LeaveRequestStatus =>\n  Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, value);\n\nexport function LeavesPage() {"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1490,"cache_read_input_tokens":283187,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1490},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3700803d-47a8-4d4a-84c0-cfce77f561c0","uuid":"b83b5e89-4e13-4308-b738-95e7db715467","timestamp":"2026-10-09T09:38:44.568Z","request_id":"req_011CfrSSnvfg46hddypsfnrX","wire_tool_inputs":{"toolu_01P7NB9vhuNKbtHx5LZY5bRx":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx","old_string":"import { NewLeaveRequestModal } from '../../components/leave';\n\nexport function LeavesPage() {","new_string":"import { NewLeaveRequestModal } from '../../components/leave';\n\n// F-015: derive the filter vocabulary from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, the exhaustive\n// Record<LeaveRequestStatus, …> that mirrors hr-service's GraphQL enum. isLeaveRequestStatus = (value: string): value is LeaveRequestStatus =>\n  Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, value);\n\nexport function LeavesPage() {","originalFile":"/**\n * Leaves Management Page\n * Displays leave requests with filtering and approval workflow\n */\n\nimport React, { useState, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { Calendar, Plus, Search, Filter, from '@aquaculture/shared-ui';\nimport {\n  useLeaveRequests,\n  usePendingLeaveApprovals,\n  useLeaveTypes,\n  useApproveLeaveRequest,\n  useRejectLeaveRequest,\n  useCurrentEmployeeId,\n} from '../../hooks';\nimport { derivePaginationMetadataV1 } from '@platform/pagination-contracts';\nimport { StatusBadge, EmployeeAvatar } from '../../components/common';\nimport type {\n  LeaveRequest,\n  LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n  LeaveRequestStatus,\n  PaginationInput,\n} from '../../types';\nimport { LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, LEAVE_CATEGORY_CONFIG } from '../../types';\n// SEC-006: sanitize API-sourced color codes before use in inline styles\nimport { sanitizeColor } from '../../components/leave/LeaveBalanceWidget';\nimport { NewLeaveRequestModal } from '../../components/leave';\n\nexport function LeavesPage() {\n  // BUG-011: use centralised hook for consistent auth→employeeId mapping\n  const employeeId = useCurrentEmployeeId();\n\n  // State\n  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'all' | 'pending' | 'mine'>('all');\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<LeaveRequestFilterInput>({});\n  const [pagination, setPagination] = useState<PaginationInput>({ limit: 20, page: 1 });\n  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');\n  const [showFilters, setShowFilters] = useState(false);\n  // BUG-010 / SEC-006: replace prompt() with controlled input state\n  const [rejectingId, setRejectingId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [showNewRequest, setShowNewRequest] = useState(false);\n  const [rejectReason, setRejectReason] = useState('');\n\n  // Data fetching\n  const { data: allRequests, isLoading: loadingAll } = useLeaveRequests(\n    activeTab === 'mine' ? { ...filter, employeeId } : filter,\n    pagination,\n  );\n  const { data: pendingApprovals, isLoading: loadingPending } =\n    usePendingLeaveApprovals(employeeId);\n  const { data: leaveTypes } = useLeaveTypes();\n\n  // Mutations\n  const approveMutation = useApproveLeaveRequest();\n  const rejectMutation = useRejectLeaveRequest();\n\n  const requests = activeTab === 'pending' ? pendingApprovals : allRequests?.items;\n  const isLoading = activeTab === 'pending' ? loadingPending : loadingAll;\n\n  // PERF-004: memoize columns — they only change when activeTab or mutation state changes\n  const columns: DataTableColumn<LeaveRequest>[] = useMemo(\n    () => [\n      {\n        key: 'employee',\n        header: 'Employee',\n        render: (_value, row) => (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n            {row.employee && (\n              <>\n                <EmployeeAvatar\n                  firstName={row.employee.firstName}\n                  lastName={row.employee.lastName}\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                />\n                <div>\n                  <p className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n                    {row.employee.firstName} {row.employee.lastName}\n                  </p>\n                  <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">{row.requestNumber}</p>\n                </div>\n              </>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        ),\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'leaveType',\n        header: 'Leave Type',\n        render: (_value, row) => {\n          const config = row.leaveType?.category\n            ? LEAVE_CATEGORY_CONFIG[row.leaveType.category]\n            : null;\n          return (\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n              {/* SEC-006: sanitize colorCode before use in inline style */}\n              <div\n                className=\"h-3 w-3 rounded-full\"\n                style={{ backgroundColor: sanitizeColor(row.leaveType?.colorCode) }}\n              />\n              <span className=\"text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">{row.leaveType?.name}</span>\n            </div>\n          );\n        },\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'dates',\n        header: 'Dates',\n        render: (_value, row) => (\n          <div className=\"text-sm\">\n            <p className=\"text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n              {new Date(row.startDate).toLocaleDateString()} -{' '}\n              {new Date(row.endDate).toLocaleDateString()}\n            </p>\n            <p className=\"text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n              {row.totalDays} day{row.totalDays !== 1 ? 's' : ''}\n              {row.isHalfDayStart && ' (half-day start)'}\n              {row.isHalfDayEnd && ' (half-day end)'}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        ),\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'status',\n        header: 'Status',\n        render: (_value, row) => {\n          const config = LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG[row.status];\n          return <StatusBadge label={config.label} variant={config.variant} size=\"sm\" />;\n        },\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'actions',\n        header: '',\n        width: '150px',\n        align: 'right',\n        render: (_value, row) => (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-2\">\n            {row.status === ('PENDING' as LeaveRequestStatus) && activeTab === 'pending' && (\n              <>\n                <Button\n                  variant=\"ghost\"\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  iconOnly\n                  aria-label=\"Approve\"\n                  onClick={(e) => {\n                    e.stopPropagation();\n                    approveMutation.mutate({ id: row.id });\n                  }}\n                  disabled={approveMutation.isPending}\n                  title=\"Approve\"\n                >\n                  <CheckCircle className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  variant=\"ghost\"\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  iconOnly\n                  aria-label=\"Reject\"\n                  onClick={(e) => {\n                    e.stopPropagation();\n                    setRejectingId(row.id);\n                    setRejectReason('');\n                  }}\n                  disabled={rejectMutation.isPending}\n                  title=\"Reject\"\n                >\n                  <XCircle className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n                </Button>\n              </>\n            )}\n            <Button\n              variant=\"ghost\"\n              size=\"sm\"\n              iconOnly\n              aria-label=\"View Details\"\n              onClick={(e) => {\n                e.stopPropagation();\n                // Open detail modal\n              }}\n              title=\"View Details\"\n            >\n              <Eye className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n            </Button>\n          </div>\n        ),\n      },\n    ],\n    [activeTab, approveMutation.isPending, rejectMutation.isPending],\n  );\n\n  // PERF-009: stable keyExtractor reference\n  const leaveKeyExtractor = useCallback((row: LeaveRequest) => row.id, []);\n\n  // BUG-012: replace any with narrower type for filter values\n  const handleFilterChange = (key: keyof LeaveRequestFilterInput, value: string | undefined) => {\n    setFilter((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      [key]: value || undefined,\n    }));\n    setPagination({ ...pagination, page: 1 });\n  };\n\n  const handlePageChange = (newPage: number) => {\n    setPagination({\n      ...pagination,\n      page: newPage,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const closeRejectModal = (): void => {\n    setRejectingId(null);\n    setRejectReason('');\n  };\n\n  const handleConfirmReject = () => {\n    if (rejectingId && rejectReason.trim()) {\n      rejectMutation.mutate(\n        { id: rejectingId, reason: rejectReason.trim() },\n        {\n          onSuccess: () => {\n            setRejectingId(null);\n            setRejectReason('');\n          },\n        },\n      );\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6 p-6\">\n      {/* Rejection reason modal — replaces prompt() */}\n      {rejectingId && (\n        <Modal\n          isOpen\n          onClose={closeRejectModal}\n          size=\"sm\"\n          title=\"Reject Leave Request\"\n          showCloseButton={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          closeOnEscape={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          closeOnOverlayClick={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          bodyClassName=\"p-6\"\n          footer={\n            <>\n              <Button variant=\"ghost\" type=\"button\" onClick={closeRejectModal}>\n                Cancel\n              </Button>\n              <Button\n                variant=\"danger\"\n                onClick={handleConfirmReject}\n                disabled={!rejectReason.trim() || rejectMutation.isPending}\n              >\n                {rejectMutation.isPending && <Spinner size=\"sm\" color=\"white\" />}\n                Confirm Rejection\n              </Button>\n            </>\n          }\n        >\n          <label\n            htmlFor=\"leave-reject-reason\"\n            className=\"mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n          >\n            Reason <span className=\"text-error-500\">*</span>\n          </label>\n          <Textarea\n            fullWidth\n            id=\"leave-reject-reason\"\n            value={rejectReason}\n            onChange={(e) => setRejectReason(e.target.value)}\n            rows={3}\n            placeholder=\"Enter the reason for rejection...\"\n            autoFocus\n          />\n        </Modal>\n      )}\n      <NewLeaveRequestModal open={showNewRequest} onClose={() => setShowNewRequest(false)} />\n      {/* Header */}\n      <PageHeader\n        title=\"Leave Management\"\n        description=\"Track and manage employee leave requests\"\n        actions={\n          <Button\n            variant=\"primary\"\n            className=\"justify-center sm:w-auto\"\n            leftIcon={<Plus className=\"h-4 w-4\" />}\n            onClick={() => setShowNewRequest(true)}\n          >\n            New Request\n          </Button>\n        }\n      />\n\n      {/* Tabs */}\n      <div className=\"flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setActiveTab('all')}\n          className={cn(\n            'border-b-2 pb-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',\n            activeTab === 'all'\n              ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400'\n              : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 hover:border-gray-300 dark:hover:border-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:hover:text-gray-100',\n          )}\n        >\n          All Requests\n        </button>\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setActiveTab('pending')}\n          className={cn(\n            'flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 pb-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',\n            activeTab === 'pending'\n              ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400'\n              : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 hover:border-gray-300 dark:hover:border-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:hover:text-gray-100',\n          )}\n        >\n          Pending Approvals\n          {pendingApprovals && pendingApprovals.length > 0 && (\n            <span className=\"rounded-full bg-warning-100 dark:bg-warning-900/40 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-warning-700 dark:text-warning-300\">\n              {pendingApprovals.length}\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </button>\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setActiveTab('mine')}\n          className={cn(\n            'border-b-2 pb-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',\n            activeTab === 'mine'\n              ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400'\n              : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 hover:border-gray-300 dark:hover:border-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:hover:text-gray-100',\n          )}\n        >\n          My Requests\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Search and Filters */}\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between\">\n        <div className=\"relative flex-1 max-w-md\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"leave-search\" className=\"sr-only\">\n            Search leave requests\n          </label>\n          <Search className=\"absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500\" />\n          <input\n            id=\"leave-search\"\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"Search requests...\"\n            value={searchQuery}\n            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}\n            className=\"w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 py-2 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm focus:border-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 dark:border-gray-600 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-white\"\n          />\n        </div>\n\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setShowFilters(!showFilters)}\n          className={cn(\n            'flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium ring-1',\n            showFilters\n              ? 'bg-primary-50 text-primary-600 ring-primary-200 dark:bg-primary-900/30 dark:text-primary-400'\n              : 'bg-white text-gray-700 ring-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200',\n          )}\n        >\n          <Filter className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n          Filters\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Filter Panel */}\n      {showFilters && (\n        <div className=\"rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 p-4 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-800/50\">\n          <div className=\"grid gap-4 grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4\">\n            <div>\n              <label\n                htmlFor=\"leave-filter-status\"\n                className=\"mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n              >\n                Status\n              </label>\n              <Select\n                fullWidth\n                options={[\n                  { value: '', label: 'All Statuses' },\n                  { value: 'pending', label: 'Pending' },\n                  { value: 'approved', label: 'Approved' },\n                  { value: 'rejected', label: 'Rejected' },\n                  { value: 'cancelled', label: 'Cancelled' },\n                ]}\n                id=\"leave-filter-status\"\n                value={filter.status || ''}\n                onChange={(e) => htmlFor=\"leave-filter-leaveType\"\n                className=\"mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n              >\n                Leave Type\n              </label>\n <option value=\"\">All Types</option>\n                {leaveTypes?.map((type) => (\n                  <option key={type.id} value={type.id}>\n                    {type.name}\n font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n              >\n                End Date\n              </label>\n              <Input\n                fullWidth\n                id=\"leave-filter-endDate\"\n                type=\"date\"\n                value={filter.endDate || ''}\n                onChange={(e) => handleFilterChange('endDate', e.target.value)}\n              />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"mt-4 flex justify-end\">\n            <Button variant=\"ghost\" onClick={() => setFilter({})}>\n              Clear all filters\n A\n// hand-copied list dropped DRAFT and WITHDRAWN and lower-cased the rest, which the\n// enum input rejects; derived, a new backend status breaks type-check at the record\n// until it has a label and then reaches this filter with no edit here. Module scope\n// keeps the array identity stable across renders.\nconst LEAVE_STATUS_FILTER_OPTIONS = [\n  { value: '', label: 'All Statuses' },\n  ...Object.entries(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG).map(([status, config]) => ({\n    value: status,\n    label: config.label,\n  })),\n];\n\n// F-015: a checked narrowing grounded in the same record, so it cannot drift from\n// the option list above.\nconst isLeaveRequestStatus = (value: string): value is LeaveRequestStatus =>\n  Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, value);\n\nexport function LeavesPage() {\n  // BUG-011: use centralised hook for consistent auth→employeeId mapping\n  const employeeId = useCurrentEmployeeId();\n\n  // State\n  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'all' | 'pending' | 'mine'>('all');\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<LeaveRequestFilterInput>({});\n  const [pagination, setPagination] = useState<PaginationInput>({ limit: 20, page: 1 });\n  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');\n  const [showFilters, setShowFilters] = useState(false);\n  // BUG-010 / SEC-006: replace prompt() with controlled input state\n  const [rejectingId, setRejectingId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [showNewRequest, setShowNewRequest] = useState(false);\n  const [rejectReason, setRejectReason] = useState('');\n\n  // Data fetching\n  const { data: allRequests, isLoading: loadingAll } = useLeaveRequests(\n    activeTab === 'mine' ? { ...filter, employeeId } : filter,\n    pagination,\n  );\n  const { data: pendingApprovals, isLoading: loadingPending } =\n    usePendingLeaveApprovals(employeeId);\n  const { data: leaveTypes } = useLeaveTypes();\n\n  // Mutations\n  const approveMutation = useApproveLeaveRequest();\n  const rejectMutation = useRejectLeaveRequest();\n\n  const requests = activeTab === 'pending' ? pendingApprovals : allRequests?.items;\n  const isLoading = activeTab === 'pending' ? loadingPending : loadingAll;\n\n  // PERF-004: memoize columns — they only change when activeTab or mutation state changes\n  const columns: DataTableColumn<LeaveRequest>[] = useMemo(\n    () => [\n      {\n        key: 'employee',\n        header: 'Employee',\n        render: (_value, row) => (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n            {row.employee && (\n              <>\n                <EmployeeAvatar\n                  firstName={row.employee.firstName}\n                  lastName={row.employee.lastName}\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                />\n                <div>\n                  <p className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n                    {row.employee.firstName} {row.employee.lastName}\n                  </p>\n                  <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">{row.requestNumber}</p>\n                </div>\n              </>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        ),\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'leaveType',\n        header: 'Leave Type',\n        render: (_value, row) => {\n          const config = row.leaveType?.category\n            ? LEAVE_CATEGORY_CONFIG[row.leaveType.category]\n            : null;\n          return (\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n              {/* SEC-006: sanitize colorCode before use in inline style */}\n              <div\n                className=\"h-3 w-3 rounded-full\"\n                style={{ backgroundColor: sanitizeColor(row.leaveType?.colorCode) }}\n              />\n              <span className=\"text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">{row.leaveType?.name}</span>\n            </div>\n          );\n        },\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'dates',\n        header: 'Dates',\n        render: (_value, row) => (\n          <div className=\"text-sm\">\n            <p className=\"text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n              {new Date(row.startDate).toLocaleDateString()} -{' '}\n              {new Date(row.endDate).toLocaleDateString()}\n            </p>\n            <p className=\"text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n              {row.totalDays} day{row.totalDays !== 1 ? key: 'status',\n        header: 'Status',\n        render: (_value, row) => {\n          const config = LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG[row.status];\n          return <StatusBadge label={config.label} => (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-2\">\n            {row.status === ('PENDING' as LeaveRequestStatus) && activeTab === 'pending' && (\n              <>\n                <Button\n                  variant=\"ghost\"\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  iconOnly\n                  aria-label=\"Approve\"\n                  onClick={(e) => {\n                    e.stopPropagation();\n                    approveMutation.mutate({ id: row.id });\n                  }}\n                  disabled={approveMutation.isPending}\n                  title=\"Approve\"\n                >\n                  <CheckCircle className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  variant=\"ghost\"\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  iconOnly\n                  aria-label=\"Reject\"\n                  onClick={(e) => {\n                    e.stopPropagation();\n                    setRejectingId(row.id);\n                    setRejectReason('');\n                  }}\n                  disabled={rejectMutation.isPending}\n                  title=\"Reject\"\n                >\n                  <XCircle className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n                </Button>\n              </>\n            )}\n            <Button\n              variant=\"ghost\"\n              size=\"sm\"\n              iconOnly\n              aria-label=\"View Details\"\n              onClick={(e) => {\n                e.stopPropagation();\n                // Open detail modal\n              }}\n              title=\"View Details\"\n            >\n              <Eye className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n            </Button>\n          </div>\n        ),\n      },\n    ],\n    [activeTab, approveMutation.isPending, rejectMutation.isPending],\n  );\n\n  // PERF-009: stable keyExtractor reference\n  const leaveKeyExtractor = useCallback((row: LeaveRequest) => row.id, []);\n\n  // BUG-012: replace any with narrower type for filter values\n  const handleFilterChange = (key: keyof LeaveRequestFilterInput, value: string | undefined) => {\n    setFilter((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      [key]: value || undefined,\n    }));\n    setPagination({ ...pagination, page: 1 });\n  };\n\n  const handlePageChange = (newPage: number) => {\n    setPagination({\n      ...pagination,\n      page: newPage,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const closeRejectModal = (): void => {\n    setRejectingId(null);\n    setRejectReason('');\n  };\n\n  const handleConfirmReject = () => {\n    if (rejectingId && rejectReason.trim()) {\n      rejectMutation.mutate(\n        { id: rejectingId, reason: rejectReason.trim() },\n        {\n          onSuccess: () => {\n            setRejectingId(null);\n            setRejectReason('');\n          },\n        },\n      );\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6 p-6\">\n      {/* Rejection reason modal — replaces prompt() */}\n      {rejectingId && (\n        <Modal\n          isOpen\n          onClose={closeRejectModal}\n          size=\"sm\"\n          title=\"Reject Leave Request\"\n          showCloseButton={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          closeOnEscape={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          closeOnOverlayClick={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          bodyClassName=\"p-6\"\n          footer={\n            <>\n              <Button variant=\"ghost\" type=\"button\" onClick={closeRejectModal}>\n                Cancel\n              </Button>\n              <Button\n                variant=\"danger\"\n                onClick={handleConfirmReject}\n                disabled={!rejectReason.trim() || rejectMutation.isPending}\n              >\n                {rejectMutation.isPending && <Spinner size=\"sm\" className=\"mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n          >\n            Reason <span className=\"text-error-500\">*</span>\n          </label>\n          <Textarea\n            fullWidth\n            id=\"leave-reject-reason\"\n            value={rejectReason}\n            onChange={(e) => setRejectReason(e.target.value)}\n            rows={3}\n  <Button\n            variant=\"primary\"\n            className=\"justify-center sm:w-auto\"\n            leftIcon={<Plus className=\"h-4 w-4\" />}\n            onClick={() =>
