Module scope\n// keeps the array identity stable across renders.\nconst LEAVE_STATUS_FILTER_OPTIONS = [\n  { value: '', label: 'All Statuses' },\n  ...Object.entries(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG).map(([status, config]) => ({\n    value: status,\n    label: config.label,\n  })),\n];\n\n// F-015: a checked narrowing grounded in the same record, so it cannot drift from\n// the option list above.\nconst isLeaveRequestStatus = (value: string): value is LeaveRequestStatus =>\n  Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, value);\n\nexport function LeavesPage() {\n  // BUG-011: use centralised hook for consistent auth→employeeId mapping\n  const employeeId = useCurrentEmployeeId();\n\n  // State\n  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'all' | 'pending' | 'mine'>('all');\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<LeaveRequestFilterInput>({});\n  const [pagination, setPagination] = useState<PaginationInput>({ limit: 20, page: 1 });\n  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');\n  const [showFilters, setShowFilters] = useState(false);\n  // BUG-010 / SEC-006: replace prompt() with controlled input state\n  const [rejectingId, setRejectingId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [showNewRequest, setShowNewRequest] = useState(false);\n  const [rejectReason, setRejectReason] = useState('');\n\n  // Data fetching\n  const { data: allRequests, isLoading: loadingAll } = useLeaveRequests(\n    activeTab === 'mine' ? { ...filter, employeeId } : filter,\n    pagination,\n  );\n  const { data: pendingApprovals, isLoading: loadingPending } =\n    usePendingLeaveApprovals(employeeId);\n  const { data: leaveTypes } = useLeaveTypes();\n\n  // Mutations\n  const approveMutation = useApproveLeaveRequest();\n  const rejectMutation = useRejectLeaveRequest();\n\n  const requests = activeTab === 'pending' ? pendingApprovals : allRequests?.items;\n  const isLoading = activeTab === 'pending' ? loadingPending : loadingAll;\n\n  // PERF-004: memoize columns — they only change when activeTab or mutation state changes\n  const columns: DataTableColumn<LeaveRequest>[] = useMemo(\n    () => [\n      {\n        key: 'employee',\n        header: 'Employee',\n        render: (_value, row) => (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n            {row.employee && (\n              <>\n                <EmployeeAvatar\n                  firstName={row.employee.firstName}\n                  lastName={row.employee.lastName}\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                />\n                <div>\n                  <p className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n                    {row.employee.firstName} {row.employee.lastName}\n                  </p>\n                  <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">{row.requestNumber}</p>\n                </div>\n              </>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        ),\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'leaveType',\n        header: 'Leave Type',\n        render: (_value, row) => {\n          const config = row.leaveType?.category\n            ? LEAVE_CATEGORY_CONFIG[row.leaveType.category]\n            : null;\n          return (\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n              {/* SEC-006: sanitize colorCode before use in inline style */}\n              <div\n                className=\"h-3 w-3 rounded-full\"\n                style={{ backgroundColor: sanitizeColor(row.leaveType?.colorCode) }}\n              />\n              <span className=\"text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">{row.leaveType?.name}</span>\n            </div>\n          );\n        },\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'dates',\n        header: 'Dates',\n        render: (_value, row) => (\n          <div className=\"text-sm\">\n            <p className=\"text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n              {new Date(row.startDate).toLocaleDateString()} -{' '}\n              {new Date(row.endDate).toLocaleDateString()}\n            </p>\n            <p className=\"text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n              {row.totalDays} day{row.totalDays !== 1 ? 's' : ''}\n              {row.isHalfDayStart && ' (half-day start)'}\n              {row.isHalfDayEnd && ' (half-day end)'}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        ),\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'status',\n        header: 'Status',\n        render: (_value, row) => {\n          const config = LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG[row.status];\n          return <StatusBadge label={config.label} variant={config.variant} size=\"sm\" />;\n        },\n      },\n      {\n        key: 'actions',\n        header: '',\n        width: '150px',\n        align: 'right',\n        render: (_value, row) => (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-2\">\n            {row.status === ('PENDING' as LeaveRequestStatus) && activeTab === 'pending' && (\n              <>\n                <Button\n                  variant=\"ghost\"\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  iconOnly\n                  aria-label=\"Approve\"\n                  onClick={(e) => {\n                    e.stopPropagation();\n                    approveMutation.mutate({ id: row.id });\n                  }}\n                  disabled={approveMutation.isPending}\n                  title=\"Approve\"\n                >\n                  <CheckCircle className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  variant=\"ghost\"\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  iconOnly\n                  aria-label=\"Reject\"\n                  onClick={(e) => {\n                    e.stopPropagation();\n                    setRejectingId(row.id);\n                    setRejectReason('');\n                  }}\n                  disabled={rejectMutation.isPending}\n                  title=\"Reject\"\n                >\n                  <XCircle className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n                </Button>\n              </>\n            )}\n            <Button\n              variant=\"ghost\"\n              size=\"sm\"\n              iconOnly\n              aria-label=\"View Details\"\n              onClick={(e) => {\n                e.stopPropagation();\n                // Open detail modal\n              }}\n              title=\"View Details\"\n            >\n              <Eye className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n            </Button>\n          </div>\n        ),\n      },\n    ],\n    [activeTab, approveMutation.isPending, rejectMutation.isPending],\n  );\n\n  // PERF-009: stable keyExtractor reference\n  const leaveKeyExtractor = useCallback((row: LeaveRequest) => row.id, []);\n\n  // BUG-012: replace any with narrower type for filter values\n  const handleFilterChange = (key: keyof LeaveRequestFilterInput, value: string | undefined) => {\n    setFilter((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      [key]: value || undefined,\n    }));\n    setPagination({ ...pagination, page: 1 });\n  };\n\n  // F-015: 'status' is a literal key here, so TypeScript checks the assigned value\n  // against LeaveRequestStatus | undefined — nothing asserts between the DOM string\n  // and the enum member GetLeaveRequests' $status variable requires.\n  const handleStatusFilterChange = (value: string): void => {\n    setFilter((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      status: isLeaveRequestStatus(value) ? value : undefined,\n    }));\n    setPagination({ ...pagination, page: 1 });\n  };\n\n  const handlePageChange = (newPage: number) => {\n    setPagination({\n      ...pagination,\n      page: newPage,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const closeRejectModal = (): void => {\n    setRejectingId(null);\n    setRejectReason('');\n  };\n\n  const handleConfirmReject = () => {\n    if (rejectingId && rejectReason.trim()) {\n      rejectMutation.mutate(\n        { id: rejectingId, reason: rejectReason.trim() },\n        {\n          onSuccess: () => {\n            setRejectingId(null);\n            setRejectReason('');\n          },\n        },\n      );\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6 p-6\">\n      {/* Rejection reason modal — replaces prompt() */}\n      {rejectingId && (\n        <Modal\n          isOpen\n          onClose={closeRejectModal}\n          size=\"sm\"\n          title=\"Reject Leave Request\"\n          showCloseButton={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          closeOnEscape={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          closeOnOverlayClick={!rejectMutation.isPending}\n          bodyClassName=\"p-6\"\n          footer={\n            <>\n              <Button variant=\"ghost\" type=\"button\" onClick={closeRejectModal}>\n                Cancel\n              </Button>\n              <Button\n                variant=\"danger\"\n                onClick={handleConfirmReject}\n                disabled={!rejectReason.trim() || rejectMutation.isPending}\n              >\n                {rejectMutation.isPending && <Spinner size=\"sm\" color=\"white\" />}\n                Confirm Rejection\n              </Button>\n            </>\n          }\n        >\n          <label\n            htmlFor=\"leave-reject-reason\"\n            className=\"mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n          >\n            Reason <span className=\"text-error-500\">*</span>\n          </label>\n          <Textarea\n            fullWidth\n            id=\"leave-reject-reason\"\n            value={rejectReason}\n            onChange={(e) => setRejectReason(e.target.value)}\n            rows={3}\n            placeholder=\"Enter the reason for rejection...\"\n            autoFocus\n          />\n        </Modal>\n      )}\n      <NewLeaveRequestModal open={showNewRequest} onClose={() => setShowNewRequest(false)} />\n      {/* Header */}\n      <PageHeader\n        title=\"Leave Management\"\n        description=\"Track and manage employee leave requests\"\n        actions={\n          <Button\n            variant=\"primary\"\n            className=\"justify-center sm:w-auto\"\n            leftIcon={<Plus className=\"h-4 w-4\" />}\n            onClick={() => setShowNewRequest(true)}\n          >\n            New Request\n          </Button>\n        }\n      />\n\n      {/* Tabs */}\n      <div className=\"flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setActiveTab('all')}\n          className={cn(\n            'border-b-2 pb-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',\n            activeTab === 'all'\n              ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400'\n              : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 hover:border-gray-300 dark:hover:border-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:hover:text-gray-100',\n          )}\n        >\n          All Requests\n        </button>\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setActiveTab('pending')}\n          className={cn(\n            'flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 pb-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',\n            activeTab === 'pending'\n              ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400'\n              : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 hover:border-gray-300 dark:hover:border-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:hover:text-gray-100',\n          )}\n        >\n          Pending Approvals\n          {pendingApprovals && pendingApprovals.length > 0 && (\n            <span className=\"rounded-full bg-warning-100 dark:bg-warning-900/40 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-warning-700 dark:text-warning-300\">\n              {pendingApprovals.length}\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </button>\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setActiveTab('mine')}\n          className={cn(\n            'border-b-2 pb-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',\n            activeTab === 'mine'\n              ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400'\n              : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 hover:border-gray-300 dark:hover:border-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:hover:text-gray-100',\n          )}\n        >\n          My Requests\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Search and Filters */}\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between\">\n        <div className=\"relative flex-1 max-w-md\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"leave-search\" className=\"sr-only\">\n            Search leave requests\n          </label>\n          <Search className=\"absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500\" />\n          <input\n            id=\"leave-search\"\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"Search requests...\"\n            value={searchQuery}\n            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}\n            className=\"w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 py-2 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm focus:border-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 dark:border-gray-600 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-white\"\n          />\n        </div>\n\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setShowFilters(!showFilters)}\n          className={cn(\n            'flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium ring-1',\n            showFilters\n              ? 'bg-primary-50 text-primary-600 ring-primary-200 dark:bg-primary-900/30 dark:text-primary-400'\n              : 'bg-white text-gray-700 ring-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200',\n          )}\n        >\n          <Filter className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n          Filters\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Filter Panel */}\n      {showFilters && (\n        <div className=\"rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 p-4 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-800/50\">\n          <div className=\"grid gap-4 grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4\">\n            <div>\n              <label\n                htmlFor=\"leave-filter-status\"\n                className=\"mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n              >\n                Status\n              </label>\n              <Select\n                fullWidth\n                options={[\n                  { value: '', label: 'All Statuses' },\n                  { value: 'pending', label: 'Pending' },\n                  { value: 'approved', label: 'Approved' },\n                  { value: 'rejected', label: 'Rejected' },\n                  { value: 'cancelled', label: 'Cancelled' },\n                ]}\n                id=\"leave-filter-status\"\n                value={filter.status || ''}\n                onChange={(e) => handleFilterChange('status', e.target.value as LeaveRequestStatus)}\n              />\n            </div>\n\n            <div>\n              <label\n                htmlFor=\"leave-filter-leaveType\"\n                className=\"mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n              >\n                Leave Type\n              </label>\n              <select\n                id=\"leave-filter-leaveType\"\n                value={filter.leaveTypeId || ''}\n                onChange={(e) => handleFilterChange('leaveTypeId', e.target.value)}\n                className=\"w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm dark:border-gray-600 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white\"\n              >\n                <option value=\"\">All Types</option>\n                {leaveTypes?.map((type) => (\n                  <option key={type.id} value={type.id}>\n                    {type.name}\n                  </option>\n                ))}\n              </select>\n            </div>\n\n            <div>\n              <label\n                htmlFor=\"leave-filter-startDate\"\n                className=\"mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n              >\n                Start Date\n              </label>\n              <Input\n                fullWidth\n font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\"\n              >\n                End Date\n              </label>\n              <Input\n                fullWidth\n                id=\"leave-filter-endDate\"\n                type=\"date\"\n                value={filter.endDate || ''}\n                onChange={(e) => handleFilterChange('endDate', e.target.value)}\n              />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"mt-4 flex justify-end\">\n            <Button variant=\"ghost\" onClick={() => setFilter({})}>\n              Clear all filters\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011vS1jvaGPRXNZwcPhuKWuX","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * WeeklySchedulePage\n3\t *\n4\t * The roster: every active employee against the visible days, each cell the\n5\t * employee's weekly-plan entry from the scheduling API. Choosing a shift or\n6\t * an off day in a cell saves it at once — the employee's plan for that week\n7\t * is created first when there is none — and the refetched overview repaints\n8\t * the cell. The daily, weekly and monthly views read the same per-week\n9\t * overviews, so an assignment made in one view is what the others show.\n10\t * Leave days come from approved leave requests and a published week is\n11\t * read-only: both are the API's rules, and the cells only reflect them.\n12\t */\n13\t\n14\timport React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';\n15\timport {\n16\t  Calendar,\n17\t  ChevronLeft,\n18\t  ChevronRight,\n19\t  Coffee,\n20\t  Lock,\n21\t  Send,\n22\t  Settings,\n23\t  Umbrella,\n24\t} from 'lucide-react';\n25\timport { Link } from 'react-router-dom';\n26\timport {\n27\t  Badge,\n28\t  Button,\n29\t  EmptyState,\n30\t  ErrorState,\n31\t  Menu,\n32\t  PageHeader,\n33\t  Spinner,\n34\t  cn,\n35\t  colors,\n36\t  useConfirm,\n37\t  type MenuItem,\n38\t} from '@aquaculture/shared-ui';\n39\t\n40\timport { sanitizeColor } from '../../components/leave/LeaveBalanceWidget';"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3700803d-47a8-4d4a-84c0-cfce77f561c0","uuid":"06122938-245a-4cb9-bd4f-4a673f2371a3","timestamp":"2026-10-09T09:39:33.009Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/scheduling/WeeklySchedulePage.tsx","content":"/**\n * WeeklySchedulePage\n *\n * The roster: every active employee against the visible days, each cell the\n * employee's weekly-plan entry from the scheduling API. Choosing a shift or\n * an off day in a cell saves it at once — the employee's plan for that week\n * is created first when there is none — and the refetched overview repaints\n * the cell. The daily, weekly and monthly views read the same per-week\n * overviews, so an assignment made in one view is what the others show.\n * Leave days come from approved leave requests and a published week is\n * read-only: both are the API's rules, and the cells only reflect them.\n */\n\nimport React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  Calendar,\n  ChevronLeft,\n  ChevronRight,\n  Coffee,\n  Lock,\n  Send,\n  Settings,\n  Umbrella,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { Link } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport {\n  Badge,\n  Button,\n  EmptyState,\n  ErrorState,\n  Menu,\n  PageHeader,\n  Spinner,\n  cn,\n  colors,\n  useConfirm,\n  type MenuItem,\n} from '@aquaculture/shared-ui';\n\nimport { sanitizeColor } from '../../components/leave/LeaveBalanceWidget';","numLines":40,"startLine":1,"totalLines":863}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QEcfPUkx8AG33uci7gUYf7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/** Versioned, browser- and server-neutral pagination authority. */\n2\texport interface PaginationResultV1<T> {\n3\t  readonly items: readonly T[];\n4\t  readonly total: number;\n5\t  readonly page: number;\n6\t  readonly limit: number;\n7\t  readonly totalPages: number;\n8\t  readonly hasNextPage: boolean;\n9\t  readonly hasPreviousPage: boolean;\n10\t}\n11\t\n12\texport type StandardPaginatedResult<T> = PaginationResultV1<T>;\n13\t\n14\t/** Pagination coordinates shared by server results and REST response metadata. */\n15\texport type PaginationMetadataV1 = Omit<PaginationResultV1<never>, 'items'>;\n16\t\n17\t/** Browser-facing projection after the REST envelope has been decoded. */\n18\texport type PaginatedDataResultV1<T> = PaginationMetadataV1 & {\n19\t  readonly data: readonly T[];\n20\t};\n21\t\n22\t/**\n23\t * Cursor pagination is a different protocol from page/limit pagination.\n24\t * Keeping it explicit prevents consumers from fabricating page coordinates\n25\t * for APIs whose only stable continuation coordinate is an opaque cursor.\n26\t */\n27\texport interface CursorPaginationResultV1<T> {\n28\t  readonly items: readonly T[];\n29\t  readonly totalCount: number;\n30\t  readonly hasMore: boolean;\n31\t  readonly cursor: string | null;\n32\t}\n33\t\n34\texport class PaginationContractError extends Error {\n35\t  constructor(\n36\t    readonly field: string,\n37\t    message: string,\n38\t  ) {\n39\t    super(`pagination.${field}: ${message}`);\n40\t    this.name = 'PaginationContractError';\n41\t  }\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\tconst issuedResults = new WeakSet<object>();\n45\tconst issuedCursorResults = new WeakSet<object>();\n46\t\n47\tfunction assertSafeInteger(value: number, field: string, minimum: number): void {\n48\t  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < minimum) {\n49\t    throw new PaginationContractError(\n50\t      field,\n51\t      `expected a safe integer greater than or equal to ${minimum}`,\n52\t    );\n53\t  }\n54\t}\n55\t\n56\texport function expectedTotalPages(total: number, limit: number): number {\n57\t  assertSafeInteger(total, 'total', 0);\n58\t  assertSafeInteger(limit, 'limit', 1);\n59\t  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / limit));\n60\t}\n61\t\n62\texport function derivePaginationMetadataV1(\n63\t  total: number,\n64\t  page: number,\n65\t  limit: number,\n66\t): PaginationMetadataV1 {\n67\t  assertSafeInteger(total, 'total', 0);\n68\t  assertSafeInteger(page, 'page', 1);\n69\t  assertSafeInteger(limit, 'limit', 1);\n70\t  const totalPages = expectedTotalPages(total, limit);\n71\t  return Object.freeze({\n72\t    total,\n73\t    page,\n74\t    limit,\n75\t    totalPages,\n76\t    hasNextPage: page < totalPages,\n77\t    hasPreviousPage: page > 1,\n78\t  });\n79\t}\n80\t\n81\texport function createStandardPaginatedResult<T>(\n82\t  items: readonly T[],\n83\t  total: number,\n84\t  page: number,\n85\t  limit: number,\n86\t): PaginationResultV1<T> {\n87\t  const metadata = derivePaginationMetadataV1(total, page, limit);\n88\t\n89\t  if (items.length > limit) {\n90\t    throw new PaginationContractError(\n91\t      'items',\n92\t      `contains ${items.length} entries, above limit ${limit}`,\n93\t    );\n94\t  }\n95\t  if (items.length > total) {\n96\t    throw new PaginationContractError(\n97\t      'items',\n98\t      `contains ${items.length} entries, above total ${total}`,\n99\t    );\n100\t  }\n101\t\n102\t  // Concurrent deletes or a narrowed filter can make a requested page stale.\n103\t  // Preserve that coordinate as an honest empty page, while rejecting data\n104\t  // that claims to exist beyond the derived final page.\n105\t  if (page > metadata.totalPages && items.length > 0) {\n106\t    throw new PaginationContractError(\n107\t      'page',\n108\t      `non-empty page exceeds totalPages ${metadata.totalPages}`,\n109\t    );\n110\t  }\n111\t\n112\t  const result: PaginationResultV1<T> = Object.freeze({\n113\t    items: Object.freeze([...items]),\n114\t    ...metadata,\n115\t  });\n116\t  issuedResults.add(result);\n117\t  return result;\n118\t}\n119\t\n120\t/**\n121\t * Recognises only values minted by createStandardPaginatedResult.\n122\t * A structurally similar object cannot silently become a wire-level page.\n123\t */\n124\texport function isStandardPaginatedResult(value: unknown): value is PaginationResultV1<unknown> {\n125\t  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && issuedResults.has(value);\n126\t}\n127\t\n128\t/**\n129\t * Detects values that claim pagination coordinates without having been issued\n130\t * by the canonical factory. Transport boundaries use this to fail closed\n131\t * instead of silently nesting a legacy or hand-written page in a plain result.\n132\t */\n133\texport function hasUnissuedPaginationShapeV1(value: unknown): boolean {\n134\t  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) {\n135\t    return false;\n136\t  }\n137\t\n138\t  const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n139\t  return (\n140\t    'total' in candidate &&\n141\t    'page' in candidate &&\n142\t    'limit' in candidate &&\n143\t    ('items' in candidate || 'data' in candidate)\n144\t  );\n145\t}\n146\t\n147\texport function paginationMetadataV1(result: PaginationResultV1<unknown>): PaginationMetadataV1 {\n148\t  if (!isStandardPaginatedResult(result)) {\n149\t    throw new PaginationContractError('authority', 'expected a factory-issued result');\n150\t  }\n151\t  return derivePaginationMetadataV1(result.total, result.page, result.limit);\n152\t}\n153\t\n154\texport function createPaginatedDataResultV1<T>(\n155\t  data: readonly T[],\n156\t  total: number,\n157\t  page: number,\n158\t  limit: number,\n159\t): PaginatedDataResultV1<T> {\n160\t  const result = createStandardPaginatedResult(data, total, page, limit);\n161\t  return Object.freeze({ data: result.items, ...paginationMetadataV1(result) });\n162\t}\n163\t\n164\texport function createCursorPaginationResultV1<T>(\n165\t  items: readonly T[],\n166\t  totalCount: number,\n167\t  hasMore: boolean,\n168\t  cursor: string | null,\n169\t): CursorPaginationResultV1<T> {\n170\t  assertSafeInteger(totalCount, 'totalCount', 0);\n171\t  if (items.length > totalCount) {\n172\t    throw new PaginationContractError(\n173\t      'items',\n174\t      `contains ${items.length} entries, above totalCount ${totalCount}`,\n175\t    );\n176\t  }\n177\t  if (hasMore !== (cursor !== null)) {\n178\t    throw new PaginationContractError('cursor', 'must be present exactly when hasMore is true');\n179\t  }\n180\t  if (cursor !== null && cursor.length === 0) {\n181\t    throw new PaginationContractError('cursor', 'must be a non-empty opaque value');\n182\t  }\n183\t\n184\t  const result: CursorPaginationResultV1<T> = Object.freeze({\n185\t    items: Object.freeze([...items]),\n186\t    totalCount,\n187\t    hasMore,\n188\t    cursor,\n189\t  });\n190\t  issuedCursorResults.add(result);\n191\t  return result;\n192\t}\n193\t\n194\texport function isCursorPaginationResultV1(\n195\t  value: unknown,\n196\t): value is CursorPaginationResultV1<unknown> {\n197\t  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && issuedCursorResults.has(value);\n198\t}\n199\t\n200\t/**\n201\t * Validates pagination metadata received across a serialization boundary.\n202\t * Redundant fields must agree with the canonical derivation.\n203\t */\n204\texport function isPaginationMetadataV1(value: unknown): value is PaginationMetadataV1 {\n205\t  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) {\n206\t    return false;\n207\t  }\n208\t\n209\t  const metadata = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n210\t  const { total, page, limit, totalPages, hasNextPage, hasPreviousPage } = metadata;\n211\t  if (\n212\t    typeof total !== 'number' ||\n213\t    !Number.isSafeInteger(total) ||\n214\t    total < 0 ||\n215\t    typeof page !== 'number' ||\n216\t Versioned, browser- and server-neutral pagination authority. */\nexport interface PaginationResultV1<T> {\n  readonly items: readonly T[];\n  readonly total: number;\n  readonly page: number;\n  readonly limit: number;\n  readonly totalPages: number;\n  readonly hasNextPage: boolean;\n  readonly hasPreviousPage: boolean;\n}\n\nexport type StandardPaginatedResult<T> = PaginationResultV1<T>;\n\n/** Pagination coordinates shared by server results and REST response metadata. */\nexport type PaginationMetadataV1 = Omit<PaginationResultV1<never>, 'items'>;\n\n/** Browser-facing projection after the REST envelope has been decoded. */\nexport type PaginatedDataResultV1<T> = PaginationMetadataV1 & {\n  readonly data: readonly T[];\n};\n\n/**\n * Cursor pagination is a different protocol from page/limit pagination.\n * Keeping it explicit prevents consumers from fabricating page coordinates\n * for APIs whose only stable continuation coordinate is an opaque cursor.\n */\nexport interface CursorPaginationResultV1<T> {\n  readonly items: readonly T[];\n  readonly totalCount: number;\n  readonly hasMore: boolean;\n  readonly cursor: string | null;\n}\n\nexport class PaginationContractError extends Error {\n  constructor(\n    readonly field: string,\n    message: string,\n  ) {\n    super(`pagination.${field}: ${message}`);\n    this.name = 'PaginationContractError';\n  }\n}\n\nconst issuedResults = new WeakSet<object>();\nconst issuedCursorResults = new WeakSet<object>();\n\nfunction assertSafeInteger(value: number, field: string, minimum: number): void {\n  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < minimum) {\n    throw new PaginationContractError(\n      field,\n      `expected a safe integer greater than or equal to ${minimum}`,\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nexport function expectedTotalPages(total: number, limit: number): number {\n  assertSafeInteger(total, 'total', 0);\n  assertSafeInteger(limit, 'limit', 1);\n  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / limit));\n}\n\nexport function derivePaginationMetadataV1(\n  total: number,\n  page: number,\n  limit: number,\n): PaginationMetadataV1 {\n  assertSafeInteger(total, 'total', 0);\n  assertSafeInteger(page, 'page', 1);\n  assertSafeInteger(limit, 'limit', 1);\n  const totalPages = expectedTotalPages(total, limit);\n  return Object.freeze({\n    total,\n    page,\n    limit,\n    totalPages,\n    hasNextPage: page < totalPages,\n    hasPreviousPage: page > 1,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function createStandardPaginatedResult<T>(\n  items: readonly T[],\n  total: number,\n  page: number,\n  limit: number,\n): PaginationResultV1<T> {\n  const metadata = derivePaginationMetadataV1(total, page, limit);\n\n  if (items.length > limit) {\n    throw new PaginationContractError(\n      'items',\n      `contains ${items.length} entries, above limit ${limit}`,\n    );\n  }\n  if (items.length > total) {\n    throw new PaginationContractError(\n      'items',\n      `contains ${items.length} entries, above total ${total}`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Concurrent deletes or a narrowed filter can make a requested page stale.\n  // Preserve that coordinate as an honest empty page, while rejecting data\n  // that claims to exist beyond the derived final page.\n  if (page > metadata.totalPages && items.length > 0) {\n    throw new PaginationContractError(\n      'page',\n      `non-empty page exceeds totalPages ${metadata.totalPages}`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  const result: PaginationResultV1<T> = Object.freeze({\n    items: Object.freeze([...items]),\n    ...metadata,\n  });\n  issuedResults.add(result);\n  return result;\n}\n\n/**\n * Recognises only values minted by createStandardPaginatedResult.\n * A structurally similar object cannot silently become a wire-level page.\n */\nexport function isStandardPaginatedResult(value: unknown): value is PaginationResultV1<unknown> {\n  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && issuedResults.has(value);\n}\n\n/**\n * Detects values that claim pagination coordinates without having been issued\n * by the canonical factory. Transport boundaries use this to fail closed\n * instead of silently nesting a legacy or hand-written page in a plain result.\n */\nexport function hasUnissuedPaginationShapeV1(value: unknown): boolean {\n  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) {\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n  return (\n    'total' in candidate &&\n    'page' in candidate &&\n    'limit' in candidate &&\n    ('items' in candidate || 'data' in candidate)\n  );\n}\n\nexport function paginationMetadataV1(result: PaginationResultV1<unknown>): PaginationMetadataV1 {\n  if (!isStandardPaginatedResult(result)) {\n    throw new PaginationContractError('authority', 'expected a factory-issued result');\n  }\n  return derivePaginationMetadataV1(result.total, result.page, result.limit);\n}\n\nexport function createPaginatedDataResultV1<T>(\n  data: readonly T[],\n  total: number,\n  page: number,\n  limit: number,\n): PaginatedDataResultV1<T> {\n  const result = createStandardPaginatedResult(data, total, page, limit);\n  return Object.freeze({ data: result.items, ...paginationMetadataV1(result) });\n}\n\nexport function createCursorPaginationResultV1<T>(\n  items: readonly T[],\n  totalCount: number,\n  hasMore: boolean,\n  cursor: string | null,\n): CursorPaginationResultV1<T> {\n  assertSafeInteger(totalCount, 'totalCount', 0);\n  if (items.length > totalCount) {\n    throw new PaginationContractError(\n      'items',\n      `contains ${items.length} entries, above totalCount ${totalCount}`,\n    );\n  }\n  if (hasMore !== (cursor !== null)) {\n    throw new PaginationContractError('cursor', 'must be present exactly when hasMore is true');\n  }\n  if (cursor !== null && cursor.length === 0) {\n    throw new PaginationContractError('cursor', 'must be a non-empty opaque value');\n  }\n\n  const result: CursorPaginationResultV1<T> = Object.freeze({\n    items: Object.freeze([...items]),\n    totalCount,\n    hasMore,\n    cursor,\n  });\n  issuedCursorResults.add(result);\n  return result;\n}\n\nexport function isCursorPaginationResultV1(\n  value: unknown,\n): value is CursorPaginationResultV1<unknown> {\n  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && issuedCursorResults.has(value);\n}\n\n/**\n * Validates pagination metadata received across a serialization boundary.\n * Redundant fields must agree with the canonical derivation.\n */\nexport function isPaginationMetadataV1(value: unknown): value is PaginationMetadataV1 {\n  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) {\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  const metadata = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n  const { total, page, limit, totalPages, hasNextPage, hasPreviousPage } = metadata;\n  if (\n    typeof total !== 'number' ||\n    !Number.isSafeInteger(total) ||\n    total < 0 ||\n    typeof page !== 'number' ||\n    !Number.isSafeInteger(page) ||\n    page < 1 ||\n    typeof limit !== 'number' ||\n    !Number.isSafeInteger(limit) ||\n    limit < 1 ||\n    typeof totalPages !== 'number' ||\n    !Number.isSafeInteger(totalPages) ||\n    typeof hasNextPage !== 'boolean' ||\n    typeof hasPreviousPage !== 'boolean'\n  ) {\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  const derived = derivePaginationMetadataV1(total, page, limit);\n  return (\n    totalPages === derived.totalPages &&\n    hasNextPage === derived.hasNextPage &&\n    hasPreviousPage === derived.hasPreviousPage\n  );\n}\n","numLines":236,"startLine":1,"totalLines":236}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfrSc2mNKo9edcWR637g6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Writing the spec (key change 2), following the cited `WeeklySchedulePage.spec.tsx` harness:"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6964,"cache_read_input_tokens":292974,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6964},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3700803d-47a8-4d4a-84c0-cfce77f561c0","uuid":"c8db0041-95f3-4f7b-a928-3a183c83ff40","timestamp":"2026-10-09T09:40:50.846Z","request_id":"req_011CfrSc2UFMrqriBkYQGj1s"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfrSc2mNKo9edcWR637g6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BNtdb9bTwrW3Bnd2VpZuLJ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/__tests__/LeavesPage.spec.tsx","content":"/**\n * LeavesPage — the status filter speaks the contract's vocabulary.\n *\n * F-015: the filter's options were hand-copied from the backend enum and drifted\n * from it twice over — DRAFT and WITHDRAWN were missing, so those requests could\n * not be filtered at all, and the four that remained were lower-case, which the\n * GraphQL enum input rejects. An `as LeaveRequestStatus` assertion on the change\n * handler kept the compiler quiet about both. These tests pin the replacement:\n * the options are DERIVED from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG — the exhaustive\n * Record<LeaveRequestStatus, …> that mirrors hr-service's enum — and a selected\n * status reaches useLeaveRequests as the enum member name the $status variable\n * requires.\n */\nimport { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\nimport type { LeaveRequest, LeaveRequestFilterInput, PaginationInput } from '../../../types';\nimport { LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG } from '../../../types';\nimport { LeavesPage } from '../LeavesPage';\n\ntype LeaveRequestsQuery = (\n  filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n  pagination?: PaginationInput,\n) => { data: { items: LeaveRequest[]; total: number }; isLoading: boolean };\n\nconst mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({\n  leaveRequests: vi.fn<LeaveRequestsQuery>(),\n}));\n\nvi.mock('../../../hooks/useLeaves', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useLeaves')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    useLeaveRequests: mocks.leaveRequests,\n    usePendingLeaveApprovals: () => ({ data: [], isLoading: false }),\n    useLeaveTypes: () => ({ data: [] }),\n    useApproveLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    useRejectLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    // The page mounts NewLeaveRequestModal whatever `open` is, so the modal's\n    // hooks run on every render too. Left live they reach the real query path and\n    // the render fails for a reason unrelated to the filter under test.\n    useCreateLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    useCalculateLeaveDays: () => ({ data: undefined, isFetching: false }),\n  };\n});\n\nvi.mock('../../../hooks/useEmployees', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useEmployees')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    useCurrentEmployeeId: () => 'employee-1',\n    useEmployees: () => ({ data: { items: [], total: 0 } }),\n  };\n});\n\n/** Renders the page, opens the filter panel and returns the status control. */\nfunction statusFilter(): HTMLSelectElement {\n  render(\n    <MemoryRouter>\n      <LeavesPage />\n    </MemoryRouter>,\n  );\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters' }));\n  const control = screen.getByLabelText('Status');\n  if (!(control instanceof HTMLSelectElement)) {\n    throw new Error('The status filter is not a native <select>');\n  }\n  return control;\n}\n\nfunction optionValues(select: HTMLSelectElement): string[] {\n  return Array.from(select.options).map((option) => option.value);\n}\n\nfunction lastQueriedFilter(): LeaveRequestFilterInput | undefined {\n  const { calls } = mocks.leaveRequests.mock;\n  return calls[calls.length - 1]?.[0];\n}\n\ndescribe('LeavesPage status filter', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    mocks.leaveRequests.mockReset();\n    mocks.leaveRequests.mockReturnValue({\n      data: { items: [], total: 0 },\n      isLoading: false,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('offers the clear-selection sentinel and all six contract statuses, none lower-case', () => {\n    expect(optionValues(statusFilter())).toEqual([\n      '',\n      'DRAFT',\n      'PENDING',\n      'APPROVED',\n      'REJECTED',\n      'CANCELLED',\n      'WITHDRAWN',\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it('derives the offered statuses from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG rather than a local copy', () => {\n    const [sentinel, ...statuses] = optionValues(statusFilter());\n\n    expect(sentinel).toBe('');\n    expect(statuses).toEqual(Object.keys(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG));\n  });\n\n  it('sends a selected status to the query as the enum member name', () => {\n    const select = statusFilter();\n\n    fireEvent.change(select, { target: { value: 'WITHDRAWN' } });\n\n    expect(lastQueriedFilter()).toEqual({ status: 'WITHDRAWN' });\n  });\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6964,"cache_read_input_tokens":292974,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6964},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3700803d-47a8-4d4a-84c0-cfce77f561c0","uuid":"582d523b-68dd-4827-905a-201a61b5da4d","timestamp":"2026-10-09T09:41:10.146Z","request_id":"req_011CfrSc2UFMrqriBkYQGj1s","wire_tool_inputs":{"toolu_01BNtdb9bTwrW3Bnd2VpZuLJ":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/__tests__/LeavesPage.spec.tsx","content":"/**\n * LeavesPage — the status filter speaks the contract's vocabulary.\n *\n * F-015: the filter's options were hand-copied from the backend enum and drifted\n * from it twice over — DRAFT and WITHDRAWN were missing, so those requests could\n * not be filtered at all, and the four that remained were lower-case, which the\n * GraphQL enum input rejects. An `as LeaveRequestStatus` assertion on the change\n * handler kept the compiler quiet about both. These tests pin the replacement:\n * the options are DERIVED from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG — the exhaustive\n * Record<LeaveRequestStatus, …> that mirrors hr-service's enum — and a selected\n * status reaches useLeaveRequests as the enum member name the $status variable\n * requires.\n */\nimport { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\nimport type { LeaveRequest, LeaveRequestFilterInput, PaginationInput } from '../../../types';\nimport { LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG } from '../../../types';\nimport { LeavesPage } from '../LeavesPage';\n\ntype LeaveRequestsQuery = (\n  filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n  pagination?: PaginationInput,\n) => { data: { items: LeaveRequest[]; total: number }; isLoading: boolean };\n\nconst mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({\n  leaveRequests: vi.fn<LeaveRequestsQuery>(),\n}));\n\nvi.mock('../../../hooks/useLeaves', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useLeaves')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    useLeaveRequests: mocks.leaveRequests,\n    usePendingLeaveApprovals: () => ({ data: [], isLoading: false }),\n    useLeaveTypes: () => ({ data: [] }),\n    useApproveLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    useRejectLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    // The page mounts NewLeaveRequestModal whatever `open` is, so the modal's\n    // hooks run on every render too. Left live they reach the real query path and\n    // the render fails for a reason unrelated to the filter under test.\n    useCreateLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    useCalculateLeaveDays: () => ({ data: undefined, isFetching: false }),\n  };\n});\n\nvi.mock('../../../hooks/useEmployees', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useEmployees')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    useCurrentEmployeeId: () => 'employee-1',\n    useEmployees: () => ({ data: { items: [], total: 0 } }),\n  };\n});\n\n/** Renders the page, opens the filter panel and returns the status control. */\nfunction statusFilter(): HTMLSelectElement {\n  render(\n    <MemoryRouter>\n      <LeavesPage />\n    </MemoryRouter>,\n  );\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters' }));\n  const control = screen.getByLabelText('Status');\n  if (!(control instanceof HTMLSelectElement)) {\n    throw new Error('The status filter is not a native <select>');\n  }\n  return control;\n}\n\nfunction optionValues(select: HTMLSelectElement): string[] {\n  return Array.from(select.options).map((option) => option.value);\n}\n\nfunction lastQueriedFilter(): LeaveRequestFilterInput | undefined {\n  const { calls } = mocks.leaveRequests.mock;\n  return calls[calls.length - 1]?.[0];\n}\n\ndescribe('LeavesPage status filter', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    mocks.leaveRequests.mockReset();\n    mocks.leaveRequests.mockReturnValue({\n      data: { items: [], total: 0 },\n      isLoading: false,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('offers the clear-selection sentinel and all six contract statuses, none lower-case', () => {\n    expect(optionValues(statusFilter())).toEqual([\n      '',\n      'DRAFT',\n      'PENDING',\n      'APPROVED',\n      'REJECTED',\n      'CANCELLED',\n      'WITHDRAWN',\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it('derives the offered statuses from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG rather than a local copy', () => {\n    const [sentinel, ...statuses] = optionValues(statusFilter());\n\n    expect(sentinel).toBe('');\n    expect(statuses).toEqual(Object.keys(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG));\n  });\n\n  it('sends a selected status to the query as the enum member name', () => {\n    const select = statusFilter();\n\n    fireEvent.change(select, { target: { value: 'WITHDRAWN' } });\n\n    expect(lastQueriedFilter()).toEqual({ status: 'WITHDRAWN' });\n  });\n});\n"}}}

An `as LeaveRequestStatus` assertion on the change\n * handler kept the compiler quiet about both. These tests pin the replacement:\n * the options are DERIVED from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG — the exhaustive\n * Record<LeaveRequestStatus, …> that mirrors hr-service's enum — and a selected\n * status reaches useLeaveRequests as the enum member name the $status variable\n * requires.\n */\nimport { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\nimport type { LeaveRequest, LeaveRequestFilterInput, PaginationInput } from '../../../types';\nimport { LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG } from '../../../types';\nimport { LeavesPage } from '../LeavesPage';\n\ntype LeaveRequestsQuery = (\n  filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n  pagination?: PaginationInput,\n) => { data: { items: LeaveRequest[]; total: number }; isLoading: boolean };\n\nconst mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({\n  leaveRequests: vi.fn<LeaveRequestsQuery>(),\n}));\n\nvi.mock('../../../hooks/useLeaves', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useLeaves')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    useLeaveRequests: mocks.leaveRequests,\n    usePendingLeaveApprovals: () => ({ data: [], isLoading: false }),\n    useLeaveTypes: () => ({ data: [] }),\n    useApproveLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    useRejectLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    // The page mounts NewLeaveRequestModal whatever `open` is, so the modal's\n    // hooks run on every render too. Left live they reach the real query path and\n    // the render fails for a reason unrelated to the filter under test.\n    useCreateLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n    useCalculateLeaveDays: () => ({ data: undefined, isFetching: false }),\n  };\n});\n\nvi.mock('../../../hooks/useEmployees', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useEmployees')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    useCurrentEmployeeId: () => 'employee-1',\n    useEmployees: () => ({ data: { items: [], total: 0 } }),\n  };\n});\n\n/** Renders the page, opens the filter panel and returns the status control. */\nfunction statusFilter(): HTMLSelectElement {\n  render(\n    <MemoryRouter>\n      <LeavesPage />\n    </MemoryRouter>,\n  );\n  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters' }));\n  const control = screen.getByLabelText('Status');\n  if (!(control instanceof HTMLSelectElement)) {\n    throw new Error('The status filter is not a native <select>');\n  }\n  return control;\n}\n\nfunction optionValues(select: HTMLSelectElement): string[] {\n  return Array.from(select.options).map((option) => option.value);\n}\n\nfunction lastQueriedFilter(): LeaveRequestFilterInput | undefined {\n  const { calls } = mocks.leaveRequests.mock;\n  return calls[calls.length - 1]?.[0];\n}\n\ndescribe('LeavesPage status filter', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    mocks.leaveRequests.mockReset();\n    mocks.leaveRequests.mockReturnValue({\n      data: { items: [], total: 0 },\n      isLoading: false,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('offers the clear-selection sentinel and all six contract statuses, none lower-case', () => {\n    expect(optionValues(statusFilter())).toEqual([\n      '',\n      'DRAFT',\n      'PENDING',\n      'APPROVED',\n      'REJECTED',\n      'CANCELLED',\n      'WITHDRAWN',\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it('derives the offered statuses from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG rather than a local copy', () => {\n    const [sentinel, ...statuses] = optionValues(statusFilter());\n\n    expect(sentinel).toBe('');\n    expect(statuses).toEqual(Object.keys(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG));\n  });\n\n  it('sends a selected status to the query as the enum member name', () => {\n    const select = statusFilter();\n\n    fireEvent.change(select, { target: { value: 'WITHDRAWN' } });\n\n    expect(lastQueriedFilter()).toEqual({ status: 'WITHDRAWN' });\n  });\n});\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Xx5NvszoKPLvbk6aLVqe6u","type":"tool_result","content":"diff --git a/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx b/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx\nindex 3e5d527b6..e81907a96 100644\n--- a/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx\n+++ b/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx\n@@ -38,6 +38,25 @@ import { LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, LEAVE_CATEGORY_CONFIG } from '../../types';\n import { sanitizeColor } from '../../components/leave/LeaveBalanceWidget';\n import { NewLeaveRequestModal } from '../../components/leave';\n \n+// F-015: derive the filter vocabulary from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, the exhaustive\n+// Record<LeaveRequestStatus, …> that mirrors hr-service's GraphQL enum. A\n+// hand-copied list dropped DRAFT and WITHDRAWN and lower-cased the rest, which the\n+// enum input rejects; derived, a new backend status breaks type-check at the record\n+// until it has a label and then reaches this filter with no edit here. Module scope\n+// keeps the array identity stable across renders.\n+const LEAVE_STATUS_FILTER_OPTIONS = [\n+  { value: '', label: 'All Statuses' },\n+  ...Object.entries(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG).map(([status, config]) => ({\n+    value: status,\n+    label: config.label,\n+  })),\n+];\n+\n+// F-015: a checked narrowing grounded in the same record, so it cannot drift from\n+// the option list above.\n+const isLeaveRequestStatus = (value: string): value is LeaveRequestStatus =>\n+  Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, value);\n+\n export function LeavesPage() {\n   // BUG-011: use centralised hook for consistent auth→employeeId mapping\n   const employeeId = useCurrentEmployeeId();\n@@ -211,6 +230,17 @@ export function LeavesPage() {\n     setPagination({ ...pagination, page: 1 });\n   };\n \n+  // F-015: 'status' is a literal key here, so TypeScript checks the assigned value\n+  // against LeaveRequestStatus | undefined — nothing asserts between the DOM string\n+  // and the enum member GetLeaveRequests' $status variable requires.\n+  const handleStatusFilterChange = (value: string): void => {\n+    setFilter((prev) => ({\n+      ...prev,\n+      status: isLeaveRequestStatus(value) ? value : undefined,\n+    }));\n+    setPagination({ ...pagination, page: 1 });\n+  };\n+\n   const handlePageChange = (newPage: number) => {\n     setPagination({\n       ...pagination,\n@@ -386,16 +416,10 @@ export function LeavesPage() {\n               </label>\n               <Select\n                 fullWidth\n-                options={[\n-                  { value: '', label: 'All Statuses' },\n-                  { value: 'pending', label: 'Pending' },\n-                  { value: 'approved', label: 'Approved' },\n-                  { value: 'rejected', label: 'Rejected' },\n-                  { value: 'cancelled', label: 'Cancelled' },\n-                ]}\n+                options={LEAVE_STATUS_FILTER_OPTIONS}\n                 id=\"leave-filter-status\"\n                 value={filter.status || ''}\n-                onChange={(e) => handleFilterChange('status', e.target.value as LeaveRequestStatus)}\n+                onChange={(e) => handleStatusFilterChange(e.target.value)}\n               />\n             </div>\n \ndiff --git a/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/__tests__/LeavesPage.spec.tsx b/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/__tests__/LeavesPage.spec.tsx\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 000000000..006ac186c\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/__tests__/LeavesPage.spec.tsx\n@@ -0,0 +1,116 @@\n+/**\n+ * LeavesPage — the status filter speaks the contract's vocabulary.\n+ *\n+ * F-015: the filter's options were hand-copied from the backend enum and drifted\n+ * from it twice over — DRAFT and WITHDRAWN were missing, so those requests could\n+ * not be filtered at all, and the four that remained were lower-case, which the\n+ * GraphQL enum input rejects. An `as LeaveRequestStatus` assertion on the change\n+ * handler kept the compiler quiet about both. These tests pin the replacement:\n+ * the options are DERIVED from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG — the exhaustive\n+ * Record<LeaveRequestStatus, …> that mirrors hr-service's enum — and a selected\n+ * status reaches useLeaveRequests as the enum member name the $status variable\n+ * requires.\n+ */\n+import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';\n+import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';\n+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n+\n+import type { LeaveRequest, LeaveRequestFilterInput, PaginationInput } from '../../../types';\n+import { LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG } from '../../../types';\n+import { LeavesPage } from '../LeavesPage';\n+\n+type LeaveRequestsQuery = (\n+  filter?: LeaveRequestFilterInput,\n+  pagination?: PaginationInput,\n+) => { data: { items: LeaveRequest[]; total: number }; isLoading: boolean };\n+\n+const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({\n+  leaveRequests: vi.fn<LeaveRequestsQuery>(),\n+}));\n+\n+vi.mock('../../../hooks/useLeaves', async (importOriginal) => {\n+  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../../hooks/useLeaves')>();\n+  return {\n+    ...actual,\n+    useLeaveRequests: mocks.leaveRequests,\n+    usePendingLeaveApprovals: () => ({ data: [], isLoading: false }),\n+    useLeaveTypes: () => ({ data: [] }),\n+    useApproveLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n+    useRejectLeaveRequest: () => ({ mutate: vi.fn(), isPending: false }),\n+    // The page mounts NewLeaveRequestModal whatever `open` is, so the modal's\n+    // hooks run on every render too. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfrTHYvRAYN3SzKsWnYTj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015aCBnvarStUdZPWsUiAPhY","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"git commit -m \"refactor(hr-module): derive the leave status filter from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG\" -m \"hr-service declares the leave-request status set once (LeaveRequestStatus, six\nmembers) and publishes it as a GraphQL enum, so only the member names are valid\ninput. The page's filter ignored that owner and hand-copied the list: it offered\nfour statuses instead of six, so DRAFT and WITHDRAWN requests could not be\nfiltered at all, and it offered them lower-cased, which the enum input rejects.\nAn 'as LeaveRequestStatus' assertion on the change handler is what hid both\ndefects from the compiler.\n\nThe options are now derived from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, the exhaustive Record over\nLeaveRequestStatus the module already owns, and that assertion is replaced by a\nmembership predicate against the same record, so the only path from the DOM into\nfilter.status is a checked narrowing. Because the record must stay exhaustive, a\nnew backend status fails type-check at its declaration until it has a label, and\nthen reaches this filter with no edit to the page: the drift becomes\nunrepresentable rather than discouraged.\n\nThe added spec pins the seven rendered option values, their equality with\nObject.keys(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG), and that selecting WITHDRAWN reaches\nuseLeaveRequests as the status the query sends.\"","description":"Commit the two staged files"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3730,"cache_read_input_tokens":315057,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3730},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3700803d-47a8-4d4a-84c0-cfce77f561c0","uuid":"ca53f08f-2680-461a-8f31-80ceb17dac21","timestamp":"2026-10-09T09:49:45.182Z","request_id":"req_011CfrTHYc4XvYigFn3iwaX1","wire_tool_inputs":{"toolu_015aCBnvarStUdZPWsUiAPhY":{"command":"git commit -m \"refactor(hr-module): derive the leave status filter from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG\" -m \"hr-service declares the leave-request status set once (LeaveRequestStatus, six\nmembers) and publishes it as a GraphQL enum, so only the member names are valid\ninput. The page's filter ignored that owner and hand-copied the list: it offered\nfour statuses instead of six, so DRAFT and WITHDRAWN requests could not be\nfiltered at all, and it offered them lower-cased, which the enum input rejects.\nAn 'as LeaveRequestStatus' assertion on the change handler is what hid both\ndefects from the compiler.\n\nThe options are now derived from LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, the exhaustive Record over\nLeaveRequestStatus the module already owns, and that assertion is replaced by a\nmembership predicate against the same record, so the only path from the DOM into\nfilter.status is a checked narrowing. Because the record must stay exhaustive, a\nnew backend status fails type-check at its declaration until it has a label, and\nthen reaches this filter with no edit to the page: the drift becomes\nunrepresentable rather than discouraged.\n\nThe added spec pins the seven rendered option values, their equality with\nObject.keys(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG), and that selecting WITHDRAWN reaches\nuseLeaveRequests as the status the query sends.\"","description":"Commit the two staged files"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfrTM5GNSSQyUt3AxJi4q","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Committed and signed (`%G?` = `G`). Working tree clean; the committed patch touches exactly the two planned files, with no formatter-hook changes beyond what I staged.\n\n**What this change does and why it matters.** One authority defines a leave-request status: the six-member enum in `apps/hr-service/src/leave/entities/leave-request.entity.ts:18`, published as a GraphQL enum at `:32`. GraphQL puts enum *member names* on the wire, so `DRAFT`…`WITHDRAWN` are the only values the `$status` variable accepts. The hr-module already mirrors that vocabulary once, in `LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG` — an exhaustive `Record<LeaveRequestStatus, …>` the page imports at `LeavesPage.tsx:36`. The filter had stopped using it and hand-typed the list at `:389`, which produced two defects at once: only four of six statuses were offered (draft and withdrawn requests were unfilterable), and those four were lower-cased, which the enum input rejects. The `as LeaveRequestStatus` assertion at `:398` is what made both invisible — it told the compiler to trust an unchecked DOM string, and the compiler obeyed.\n\nThe fix is Tier 1 rather than a corrected list: options are now *derived* from the record, and the assertion is replaced by a `value is LeaveRequestStatus` predicate testing membership in that same record, so the only route from the DOM into `filter.status` is a checked narrowing against a literal key. **If this were skipped**, every explicit status selection would keep failing against the contract, draft and withdrawn would stay unreachable, and the next backend status would re-open the same drift with no build signal. Because the record must stay exhaustive, a new enum member now breaks `npm run type-check` at the record's declaration until it has a label — and once labelled it reaches the filter with no edit to the page. Downstream surface: the hr-module leave-list route rendered inside `web/shell`, and the hr-service resolver receiving `$status`.\n\nOne wiring fact worth recording for whoever reads the spec next: `LeavesPage` mounts `NewLeaveRequestModal` unconditionally, so that modal's hooks (`useEmployees`, `useCreateLeaveRequest`, `useCalculateLeaveDays`) execute on every render regardless of `open`. Left live they reach a real query path and the render fails for a reason unrelated to the filter — the failure mode risk P-R-002 names. They are stubbed inside the same two mocked hook modules the plan declares.\n\nOne ambiguity to flag: this request printed no `claim_id`, so the envelope below carries the request id in that field; the executor's own claim identity governs.\n\n```json\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n  \"request_id\": \"AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678\",\n  \"claim_id\": \"AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678\",\n  \"agent_id\": \"aria-implementer\",\n  \"role\": \"implementation\",\n  \"status\": \"submitted\",\n  \"output_path\": \"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/.aria-state-store/tools/agent-invocations/outputs/plan-cyc-20261008T175911Z-auto/round-0-implementation-AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678.md\",\n  \"satisfaction_matrix\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"authenticity:plan-cyc-20261008T175911Z-auto\",\n      \"verdict\": \"satisfied\",\n      \"note\": \"Verified with one mcp__aria__plan_verify call for {plan_id: plan-cyc-20261008T175911Z-auto, content_hash: sha256:c69948ca25137b0c471a6474a6e644f89b62ec9eab9e0ee05b8f069d57501602}. The kernel recomputed the hash from its own ledger and answered verdict=verified, content_hash equal to the claimed hash, revision_id rev-plan-cyc-20261008T175911Z-auto-r1-e798215a0476. The hash was not recomputed by hand. Branch and base were confirmed before any edit: git branch --show-current printed aria-impl-7c52b7f387ab21ab5550aa4148a71306 and git rev-parse HEAD printed 83cc73af44cfa6d6be11acadf89dbf8e2689e259, both equal to implementation_ids.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"key_change:0\",\n      \"verdict\": \"satisfied\",\n      \"note\": \"All four prescribed edits applied inside web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx only. (1) At module scope LEAVE_STATUS_FILTER_OPTIONS derives the list from the existing record, sentinel first then Object.entries(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG) in declaration order; it replaces the literal array at the former lines 389-395. (2) At module scope isLeaveRequestStatus is a value is LeaveRequestStatus predicate over Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG, value). (3) In the component handleStatusFilterChange assigns the literal status key, so the compiler checks the value against LeaveRequestStatus | undefined with no assertion, and resets pagination to page 1 exactly as the existing handler does. (4) The Select onChange now calls handleStatusFilterChange(e.target.value) and the as LeaveRequestStatus assertion is deleted with nothing replacing it. Unchanged as instructed: handleFilterChange still serves leaveTypeId, startDate and endDate, and value={filter.status || ''} is untouched. No second status list, label map or colour map was introduced; LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG remains the single owner. web/shared-ui and the hr-service entity were read only. The pre-existing assertion at LeavesPage.tsx:149 lies outside this key change and was deliberately left alone.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx:389\",\n        \"web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx:398\",\n        \"web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx:36\",\n        \"web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx:33\",\n        \"web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/LeavesPage.tsx:206\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/leave/entities/leave-request.entity.ts:18\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/leave/entities/leave-request.entity.ts:32\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"key_change:1\",\n      \"verdict\": \"satisfied\",\n      \"note\": \"Added web/modules/hr-module/src/pages/leaves/__tests__/LeavesPage.spec.tsx on the harness the cited scheduling page spec uses: explicit vitest imports, a vi.hoisted() mock handle, vi.mock(module, importOriginal) partial mocks, and render inside MemoryRouter. Both declared hook modules are mocked: ../../../hooks/useLeaves (useLeaveRequests, usePendingLeaveApprovals, useLeaveTypes, useApproveLeaveRequest, useRejectLeaveRequest) and ../../../hooks/useEmployees (useCurrentEmployeeId). The page imports these through the ../../hooks barrel, which re-exports from the same resolved modules, so the partial mocks take effect. The spec clicks the Filters toggle and reads the control with getByLabelText('Status'); the page passes no placeholder prop, so Select renders exactly one option per entry and adds no disabled option. The three required assertions are present: (a) option values equal ['', 'DRAFT', 'PENDING', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'CANCELLED', 'WITHDRAWN'] in that order, seven values, none lower-case; (b) the non-sentinel values equal Object.keys(LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG); (c) firing a change to WITHDRAWN re-invokes useLeaveRequests with filter.status equal to WITHDRAWN. Two hooks beyond the named list are stubbed in the same two mocked modules because LeavesPage mounts NewLeaveRequestModal whatever its open prop is, so useCreateLeaveRequest, useCalculateLeaveDays and useEmployees execute on every render; left live the render reaches a live query path and fails for a cause unrelated to the filter under test. and signed (`%G?` = `G`). Working tree clean; the committed patch touches exactly the two planned files, with no formatter-hook changes beyond what I staged.\n\n**What this change does and why it matters.** One authority defines a leave-request status: the six-member enum in `apps/hr-service/src/leave/entities/leave-request.entity.ts:18`, published as a GraphQL enum at `:32`. GraphQL puts enum *member names* on the wire, so `DRAFT`…`WITHDRAWN` are the only values the `$status` variable accepts. The hr-module already mirrors that vocabulary once, in `LEAVE_STATUS_CONFIG` — an exhaustive `Record<LeaveRequestStatus, …>` the page imports at `LeavesPage.tsx:36`. The filter had stopped using it and hand-typed the list at `:389`, which produced two defects at once: only four of six statuses were offered (draft and withdrawn requests were unfilterable), and those four were lower-cased, which the enum input rejects. The `as LeaveRequestStatus` assertion at `:398` is what made both invisible — it told the compiler to trust an unchecked DOM string, and the compiler obeyed.\n\nThe fix is Tier 1 rather than a corrected list: options are now *derived* from the record, and the assertion is replaced by a `value is LeaveRequestStatus` predicate testing membership in that same record, so the only route from the DOM into `filter.status` is a checked narrowing against a literal key. **If this were skipped**, every explicit status selection would keep failing against the contract, draft and withdrawn would stay unreachable, and the next backend status would re-open the same drift with no build signal. Because the record must stay exhaustive, a new enum member now breaks `npm run type-check` at the record's declaration until it has a label — and once labelled it reaches the filter with no edit to the page. Downstream surface: the hr-module leave-list route rendered inside `web/shell`, and the hr-service resolver receiving `$status`.\n\nOne wiring fact worth recording for whoever reads the spec next: `LeavesPage` mounts `NewLeaveRequestModal` unconditionally, so that modal's hooks (`useEmployees`, `useCreateLeaveRequest`, `useCalculateLeaveDays`) execute on every render regardless of `open`. Left live they reach a real query path and the render fails for a reason unrelated to the filter — the failure mode risk P-R-002 names. They are stubbed inside the same two mocked hook modules the plan declares.\n\nOne ambiguity to flag: this request printed no `claim_id`, so the envelope below carries the request id in that field; the executor's own claim identity governs.\n\n```json\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n  \"request_id\": \"AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678\",\n  \"claim_id\": \"AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678\",\n  \"agent_id\": \"aria-implementer\",\n  \"role\": \"implementation\",\n  \"status\": \"submitted\",\n  \"output_path\": \"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/.aria-state-store/tools/agent-invocations/outputs/plan-cyc-20261008T175911Z-auto/round-0-implementation-AIR-aria-implementer-9f5893f12678.md\",\n  \"satisfaction_matrix\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"authenticity:plan-cyc-20261008T175911Z-auto\",\n      \"verdict\": \"satisfied\",\n      \"note\": \"Verified with one mcp__aria__plan_verify call for {plan_id: plan-cyc-20261008T175911Z-auto, content_hash: sha256:c69948ca25137b0c471a6474a6e644f89b62ec9eab9e0ee05b8f069d57501602}. (3) In the component handleStatusFilterChange assigns the literal status key, so the compiler checks the value against LeaveRequestStatus | undefined with no assertion, and resets pagination to page 1 exactly as the existing handler does. (4) The Select onChange now calls handleStatusFilterChange(e.target.value) and the as LeaveRequestStatus assertion is deleted with nothing replacing it.
